# TCP Facts 2026-10-14

1621 checked facts about T Cloud Public (TCP). One line per fact, grouped by topic. Read the comment at the top before answering.

## ecs (164)

- Each ECS flavor exposes info:cpu:name in os_extra_specs, giving CPU microarchitecture and clock, for example Intel Cascade Lake 2.6GHz. The newest s9 and c9 families return masked values such as Intel CPU N.NGHz or General Purpose Processor. (KB-232P, live, checked 2026-07-19)
- The service description contracts an availability SLA for ECS, EVS and OBS. Periods in which at least one AZ of the region stays up are excluded from the downtime calculation. (KB-23F7, contract, checked 2026-09-16)
- The ECS flavor listing at /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/flavors in eu-de returns os_extra_specs per flavor. cond:operation:az holds the state per AZ, for example eu-de-01(sellout),eu-de-03(normal), with the values normal, sellout and abandon. The per-AZ value overrides the top-level cond:operation:status, so status abandon with eu-de-01(normal) still means orderable in eu-de-01. (KB-25LL, live, checked 2026-06-11)
- A Windows ECS created with adminPass instead of key_name demands a password change at first logon, and with NLA active RDP then fails with 0x1207. With key_name, the password is read from GET /v2.1/{project_id}/servers/{id}/os-server-password, base64-decoded and decrypted with the private key. (KB-2K2K, live, checked 2026-06-11)
- The default ECS quotas in eu-de are maxTotalInstances 100, maxTotalCores 800 and maxTotalRAMSize 2500000 MB, about 2441 GiB. They are read with GET /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/limits and GET /v1/{project_id}/quotas. (KB-2QQ4, live, checked 2026-08-25)
- os-getConsoleOutput returns only the tail of the serial console log when a length is passed, and the log can lag behind the finish of cloud-init. Requesting the full output without a length parameter returns earlier lines that the tail is missing. (KB-2SFK, live, checked 2026-07-17)
- Starting a stopped standby ECS instance took 11 s with a Debian guest and 23 s with a Windows Server 2022 guest. (KB-2XII, live, checked 2026-09-02)
- The storage optimized ECS families i3 and i3m carry local NVMe disks: i3 one to eight disks of 3,200 GiB, i3m one to seven of 1,600 GiB. The service description revision 17.08.2026 offers both only in EU-NL, and on 2026-09-25 the flavor list of eu-nl carried 10 i3 and 6 i3m flavors while eu-de carried none. Section 6.1.1 says the i3 and d2 flavors and all dedicated host types are also charged in the shutdown state. (KB-3744, live, checked 2026-09-25)
- os-getConsoleOutput returns HTTP 200 with empty output for an ECS that does not actively write to ttyS0, which is typical for imported images. The same call returns text for a guest that writes to ttyS0 from user_data. (KB-3ECJ, live, checked 2026-06-12)
- The price API lists the ECS flavor families under six service names: ecs (s2, s3, s7n, s9), ecsflex (x1, x1e, x1m, x2, x2e, x2m, x2ne), ecsnoc (c4, c4ne, c7n, c7t, c9), memo (m4, m7n, m9), lms (e3) and gpu (the GPU families). A search under sn=ecs alone misses most families, and m3 appears only among the MRS node prices. (KB-3KHR, live, checked 2026-09-24)
- ECS auto-recovery appears in CTS as the trace names addAutoRecovery and deleteAutoRecovery. These record the configuration change, not a recovery event. (KB-3RWJ, live, checked 2026-08-26)
- On 2026-09-25 every s9 size (21 sizes, s9.medium.1 to s9.16xlarge.4) read normal in cond:operation:az for eu-de-01, eu-de-02 and eu-de-03. (KB-3TBJ, live, checked 2026-09-25)
- A resize from s9 back to an older general-purpose family is refused with Ecs.0804, current flavor can not resize to flavor, so the rollback path is a restore from snapshot or backup. (KB-3XVN, live, checked 2026-07-21)
- An ECS resize keeps the server in its original availability zone, so the target flavor has to have capacity in that same AZ. (KB-47AN, live, checked 2026-07-21)
- A Linux ECS started from an Ubuntu public image has the default login user ubuntu and is set up for key-pair SSH login. (KB-47ME, live, checked 2026-06-15)
- POST /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/delete on the ECS endpoint takes the fields delete_publicip and delete_volume. With delete_publicip false and delete_volume true the server, its system disk and its data disks are deleted, and the elastic IP stays in the account as unbound. (KB-4BMR, live, checked 2026-06-17)
- An ECS create on c7n.large.2 in eu-de ignores hw:passthrough true in root_volume: the system disk still attaches as a virtio-blk disk (/dev/vda, bus virtio). (KB-4KZN, live, checked 2026-10-01)
- An ECS create on c7n.large.2 in eu-de from an image with hw_disk_bus scsi and hw_scsi_model virtio-scsi fails: the job ends in Ecs.0204 with 'server is error, code is 0107012' and TCP rolls the server back. (KB-4XFL, live, checked 2026-10-01)
- The ECS batch delete call POST /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/delete takes the flags delete_volume and delete_publicip. With delete_publicip set to false the attached EIP stays allocated and is released separately with DELETE /v1/{project_id}/publicips/{eip_id}. (KB-5SA7, live, checked 2026-07-09)
- An ECS create in eu-de from an image with hw_disk_bus sata fails on c7n.large.2, c9.large.2, s9.large.2 and s7n.large.2: the job ends in Ecs.0204 with 'server is error, code is 0300000' and TCP rolls the server back. (KB-5YJ7, live, checked 2026-10-01)
- The web console limits a pasted public key to 1024 characters when a key pair is imported. The key pair API accepts a key longer than that. (KB-62HO, live, checked 2026-02-04)
- A root volume created inline with an ECS carries delete_on_termination true, which overrides delete_volume false at teardown and removes the disk. The current value shows in os-extended-volumes:volumes_attached[].delete_on_termination on the server. (KB-6C7X, live, checked 2026-07-29)
- An ECS on c7n.large.2 in eu-de that boots from a UEFI image runs OVMF on the QEMU machine type pc-i440fx-4.1, with its system disk on virtio-blk. (KB-6KHR, live, checked 2026-10-01)
- Upstream OpenStack Nova accepts sata as a disk bus for the libvirt virt types kvm and qemu (SUPPORTED_DEVICE_BUSES lists virtio, scsi, ide, usb, fdc and sata), so hw_disk_bus=sata is a standard Nova option that TCP refuses with code 0300000. (KB-6MAQ, docs, checked 2026-10-09)
- Flavor sellability has to be read from the per-AZ field cond:operation:az. The global cond:operation:status can report abandon for a flavor that is still sellable in an availability zone. (KB-6PLG, live, checked 2026-06-12)
- An ECS create can return error Ecs.0319 with the message "capacity not sufficient", which reports a stock shortage of that flavor. A flavor listed with os_extra_specs["cond:operation:az"] = normal is orderable and can still return Ecs.0319. (KB-6VPI, live, checked 2026-08-18)
- An ECS can read its own server id from the instance metadata service at /openstack/latest/meta_data.json. That id is what ECS API calls need to address the instance itself. (KB-7AIC, live, checked 2026-05-01)
- In the console the same setting is the Source/Destination Check toggle, reached on the ECS detail page under the Network Interfaces tab by opening the NIC's IP details. (KB-7HLP, docs, checked 2026-07-23)
- An ECS create issued while the subnet is still in state BUILD fails. Polling the VPC to status OK and the subnet to status ACTIVE first makes the same create succeed. (KB-7LLJ, live, checked 2026-08-18)
- The flavor cond:image support tags __support_kvm and __support_kvm_hi1822_hiovs are enforced by the console flavor picker only. With the hi1822 tag stripped from a private image, an s9 create and a resize from s3 to s9 both returned HTTP 200 and the instance booted ACTIVE. (KB-A4NI, live, checked 2026-07-20)
- The ECS api-ref documents the extra spec ecs:generation. The digit in a flavor name gives the generation, for example s3 is general computing III. (KB-A7FI, docs, checked 2026-09-02)
- When an ECS is created with POST /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers, as after an image import or from a full-ECS image, an empty security_groups binds the default security group, an empty ip_address takes a free address of the subnet and a missing availability_zone lets the system pick the AZ. These three settings come from the create call, not from the image. (KB-AARL, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- GET /v2.1/{project_id}/servers/{id}/os-server-password returns an empty body, not an error, until Cloudbase-Init finishes the first boot of a key-pair Windows ECS. The base64 RSA ciphertext then appears and is decrypted with the key-pair private key. (KB-AEHL, live, checked 2026-09-25)
- POST /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/delete with delete_volume true and delete_publicip false removes an ECS with its data disks and leaves the EIP unbound. PUT /v1/{project_id}/publicips/{id} with a publicip.port_id taken from /cloudservers/{server_id}/os-interface rebinds that EIP to another server. (KB-AJER, live, checked 2026-06-17)
- When an ECS is created from a full-ECS image, the data disks of the image are restored without being listed in data_volumes, and each data_volumes entry without extendparam.snapshotId adds a new disk. Per the API reference, an entry that carries the snapshotId of an image data disk changes that disk instead of adding one. (KB-AZ3P, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- On 2026-10-04 in eu-de the A40 flavor g7.12xlarge.8 (1 x A40 48G) read normal in eu-de-01 and eu-de-03, g7.24xlarge.8 (2 x A40) normal in eu-de-03 and sellout in eu-de-01, and the vGPU sizes g7v.2xlarge.8 and g7v.4xlarge.8 normal in eu-de-01. A normal zone state is a catalog state, not a stock guarantee. (KB-B4YD, live, checked 2026-10-04)
- The source and destination check and the EIP binding are properties of the port, not of the guest, so a rebuilt instance needs both applied again on its new ports, the EIP bound by port_id. (KB-B7RT, live, checked 2026-07-29)
- POST /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/{id}/resize is asynchronous: it returns a job_id and the instance goes to status RESIZE. A start issued before GET /v1/{project_id}/jobs/{job_id} reports SUCCESS leaves the instance on its old flavor. A refused resize returns no job_id. (KB-BACI, live, checked 2026-07-21)
- In the eu-de ECS flavor catalog, os_extra_specs["cond:operation:az"] carries per-AZ orderability with the values normal, sellout and abandon. The region-wide os_extra_specs["cond:operation:status"] reads abandon on 365 of 372 flavors, 327 of them normal in some AZ, so it does not separate orderable flavors (2026-09-25). (KB-BECR, live, checked 2026-09-25)
- The private key material of a key pair created through the API is returned once, in the body of the create response. The key pair therefore has to exist before the ECS create that references it. (KB-BFKU, live, checked 2026-08-18)
- The service description (revision 17.08.2026) lists physical Elastic Cloud Server flavors c9.144xl.2, c9.144xl.4 and m9.144xl.8 with 384 vCPU (c9.144xl.4 with 1536 GiB RAM), against 192 vCPU for the largest c9 and m9 virtual flavors and 208 for the e3 large-memory family. They are expected available from 01.10.2026 and offered on request only. (KB-BJGL, contract, checked 2026-09-25)
- The console Get Password section and the API GET /v2.1/{project_id}/servers/{server_id}/os-server-password apply to instances created with a key pair, where Cloudbase-Init generates a random password and encrypts it with the public key. An instance created with adminPass shows no Get Password section. (KB-CBPS, live, checked 2026-06-10)
- os-getConsoleOutput on an ECS in eu-de that is still in BUILD (task_state spawning) answers HTTP 404 itemNotFound with 'Instance instance-... could not be found'. A few seconds later, still in spawning, the same call returns HTTP 200 with an empty output, and the boot lines follow once the server is ACTIVE. (KB-CF4H, live, checked 2026-10-07)
- An ECS is created with POST /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers, which returns job_id and serverIds; the ECS jobs endpoint is polled until the server is ACTIVE. In the nics array the field subnet_id is the VPC v1 subnet id, and root_volume size must be at least the image min_disk. (KB-CYMN, live, checked 2026-07-20)
- Console output timing depends on the host generation. c9 hosts wrap the lines in qemu-kvm charserial0 log prefixes and can lag minutes behind the guest, leaving the console empty long after boot, while s7n hosts deliver clean lines quickly. (KB-CZPT, live, checked 2026-06-11)
- The Swiss T Cloud Public (eu-ch2) differs from eu-de per the Cloud Create guide: only s3 flavors (s3.medium.1 and the like), two availability zones eu-ch2a and eu-ch2b, EVS volume types SAS and SSD only, DNS private zones only (no public zones) and NAT gateway specifications of its own. Its documentation lives on a Swiss documentation portal of its own. (KB-DVB7, docs, checked 2026-10-08)
- Attaching an EVS volume to a running ECS goes through the ECS API as POST /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/{server_id}/attachvolume. The call is asynchronous: it returns a job that has to be polled on the ECS jobs endpoint until the job state reads SUCCESS. (KB-DWRX, live, checked 2026-05-01)
- GET /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/flavors with availability_zone=eu-de-01 returned 326 flavors, 323 of them carrying os_extra_specs cond:operation:status abandon, among them s2 and s3 families that were orderable at that time. (KB-DXPP, live, checked 2026-08-26)
- The system disk of a new ECS must be at least the min_disk of its source image. Private images with preinstalled software carried min_disk 200 GB, so a 120 GB system disk required a fresh public image instead. (KB-E6CH, live, checked 2026-06-17)
- ECS quota increases go through the service hotline or email, per the ECS documentation on quota adjustment, so a large estate needs the request filed well before deployment. (KB-EOFG, docs, checked 2026-08-25)
- T Cloud Public (TCP) provides virtual machines through Elastic Cloud Server (ECS) with Microsoft Windows Server and Linux operating systems: the public images include Windows Server, SUSE Linux Enterprise Server, Oracle Linux and Red Hat Enterprise Linux, and customers can also bring their own private images. Windows Server 2025 and Ubuntu, RHEL and other public images were listed live in eu-de on 2026-09-25 (KB-M3JR, KB-GP36). (KB-EPEA, contract, checked 2026-10-11)
- PUT /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/{server_id}/os-reset-password on the ECS endpoint in eu-de returns 404 with error code APIGW.0101, The API does not exist or has not been published in the environment. The one-click Windows password reset of the upstream API is not published on T Cloud Public (TCP). (KB-F25K, live, checked 2026-08-24)
- os-getConsoleOutput returned an empty output for public Ubuntu 24.04 guests on s9.large.2 in eu-de, while c7n.large.2 guests returned their kernel log within a minute. (KB-F27E, live, checked 2026-10-01)
- A noVNC console URL for an ECS is returned by POST /v2.1/{project_id}/servers/{server_id}/action with the body {"os-getVNCConsole": {"type": "novnc"}}, on the host console.otc.t-systems.com (2026-09-25). A test in February found the URL valid for about five minutes. The ECS remote_console paths under v1 and v1.1 return HTTP 404. (KB-F6JF, live, checked 2026-09-25)
- The port_id of each NIC is read from the ECS server detail response under addresses, keyed by vpc id, in the element field OS-EXT-IPS:port_id. The array order follows the order of the nics array at creation, so index 0 is port1. (KB-FNJH, live, checked 2026-07-23)
- The U-Boot of the Meraki vMX 26.1.4 KVM image sets stdin=serial and stdout=serial,vidconsole with bootdelay -2. Its prompt shows on the VNC console but takes keyboard input only from the serial port, so typing in noVNC on TCP does nothing. (KB-FP7F, live, checked 2026-10-07)
- In the v1.1 ECS create call each volume carries a metadata object with __system__encrypted set to "1" and __system__cmkid set to a KMS key id. System and data volumes are encrypted independently, and the result shows in the EVS volumes/{volume_id} metadata. (KB-FQNN, live, checked 2026-06-15)
- The physical flavor c7t.28xlarge.4.physical (112 vCPUs, 512 GiB) provides Intel Software Guard Extensions (SGX) for secure enclaves, and the ECS user guide adds trusted boot measured from a TPM chip for C7t. The service description revision 17.08.2026 lists c7t only in EU-DE, and on 2026-09-25 the flavor list of eu-de carried it while the one of eu-nl did not. (KB-G6SH, live, checked 2026-09-25)
- Default ECS quotas from GET /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/limits are 100 instances, 800 vCPUs and 2,500,000 MB RAM (about 2,441 GiB); the default VPC public IP quota is 10. That is about 3 GiB per vCPU, so an estate averaging more RAM per vCPU hits the RAM quota before the vCPU quota. (KB-GBRH, live, checked 2026-08-25)
- The ECS changeos call (POST /v2/{project_id}/cloudservers/{server_id}/changeos) accepted adminpass instead of keyname for a Windows ECS on 2026-09-25, together with mode withStopServer, and its job ended in SUCCESS. The API reference lists keyname as mandatory and no adminpass. Whether the new password took effect was not checked, because that needs a login. (KB-GL5C, live, checked 2026-09-25)
- For ECS flavour availability per zone the field os_extra_specs cond:operation:az is authoritative, with values such as eu-de-01(normal),eu-de-02(normal),eu-de-03(normal). The global cond:operation:status is the fallback only for a zone that is absent from that per-AZ field. (KB-GSI6, live, checked 2026-08-26)
- The guest serial console is readable through the ECS API: POST /v2.1/{project_id}/servers/{server_id}/action with the os-getConsoleOutput action and a length field returns what the guest wrote to /dev/ttyS0, so a guest with no reachable network can still report. (KB-GYKQ, live, checked 2026-06-11)
- A root volume created inside the ECS create call (root_volume block in POST /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers) carries delete_on_termination true on its attachment. Passing delete_volume false on POST /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/delete does not override it, and the volume is destroyed with the instance. (KB-GZ52, live, checked 2026-07-29)
- In an ECS flavor's extra specs cond:operation:az overrides cond:operation:status per availability zone. The format is az(xx), the value in the parentheses is the flavor status in that AZ, and empty parentheses make the entry invalid. The top-level status applies only to AZs the parameter does not list. (KB-H4FB, docs, checked 2026-09-02)
- Apache Iceberg runs self-managed on a plain ECS with Spark 3.3.1, iceberg-spark-runtime-3.3_2.12 1.4.3 and hadoop-aws 3.3.2 over OBS via S3A. It passed 15 of 15 functional checks, including MERGE, row-level UPDATE and DELETE, time travel, schema and partition evolution and compaction, with Hadoop and JDBC catalogs. (KB-HAQG, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- An ECS created from a shared Windows Server 2022 image on flavor s7n.large.4 was resized through the API to s9.large.2 successfully. (KB-HAVV, live, checked 2026-07-21)
- A flavor resize can fail with HTTP 400 Ecs.0319, capacity not sufficient, even when the flavor is listed with cond:operation:az=normal. An orderable flavor status does not imply capacity in the availability zone at that moment. (KB-HEJ4, live, checked 2026-07-21)
- The ECS flavor listing in eu-de returned 372 flavors on 2026-09-25, 332 of them normal in at least one AZ. (KB-HIYO, live, checked 2026-09-25)
- A Windows ECS administrator password must be 8 to 26 characters long, use three character classes and must not contain the user name. (KB-HKXY, docs, checked 2026-06-10)
- A local administrator account created in Windows ECS user_data with -UserMayNotChangePassword and added to the Administrators group authenticates over RDP on T Cloud Public (TCP) without an interactive password change. (KB-IAIS, live, checked 2026-08-24)
- In the eu-de ECS flavor catalogue cond:operation:status=abandon is set on nearly every flavor family, including s9, c9 and x1, while the per-AZ field cond:operation:az of the same flavors reports states such as eu-de-01(normal). (KB-IC4C, live, checked 2026-06-11)
- A private IP address can be moved between availability zones by hand because subnets are not bound to one zone: delete the server and create it in another AZ with nics[].ip_address set to the same address, 33 seconds. The address is free only once the old server is really deleted. (KB-IJOA, live, checked 2026-09-02)
- A guest on c7n.large.2 in eu-de sees the CPU brand string Intel(R) Xeon(R) Gold 6348 CPU @ 2.60GHz, family 6, model 106, stepping 6, with two threads per core. (KB-IMA2, live, checked 2026-10-01)
- A public Ubuntu 24.04 image in eu-de takes its packages from debmirror.eu-de.otc-service.com. An ECS on a subnet with the internal DNS server installed qemu-system-x86 and ovmf from it without an EIP. (KB-IT7E, live, checked 2026-10-01)
- First boot of a newly created Windows Server machine measured 213 s and 269 s in two runs on Windows Server 2022 and 235 s on Windows Server 2019, which was the only figure taken on 2019. (KB-IWPS, live, checked 2026-09-02)
- POST /v2/{project_id}/servers/{server_id}/action with body {"os-getConsoleOutput": {"length": 200}} returns the guest console output of an ECS instance, which shows whether a user_data script ran inside the guest. (KB-J4VQ, live, checked 2026-08-18)
- An ECS in eu-de accepts a CD-ROM through block_device_mapping_v2 with device_type=cdrom and disk_bus=ide on the native v2.1 create API. The guest gets /dev/sr0, a 1 GB ATA CD-ROM on an ata/AHCI controller (ata1), while the system disk stays virtio-blk. A blank volume backs it. (KB-K76G, live, checked 2026-10-02)
- On 2026-07-19 the ECS flavor listing in eu-de reported Intel SkyLake for x1 and x1e, Cascade Lake 2.6GHz for s3, x2 and x2ne, Cascade Lake 3.0GHz for x2e, c4 and c4ne, Ice Lake 2.6GHz for s7n and c7n, and masked names for s9 and c9. (KB-KFQU, live, checked 2026-07-19)
- In eu-de the EVS volume type SATA (common I/O, end-of-sale) is marked sold out in eu-de-01, eu-de-02 and eu-de-03 (read 2026-09-25 and again 2026-10-12), while SAS, SSD, GPSSD, GPSSD2 and ESSD carry no sold-out mark. The mark is the key os-vendor-extended:sold_out_availability_zones inside extra_specs of GET evs /v2/{project_id}/types, one comma-separated string of zone names; a type on sale has no such key. An ECS create with root_volume.volumetype SATA in eu-de-01 answers HTTP 400 with error_code Ecs.0044 and the message that the volume type is sold out in that AZ (live check on a test tenant, 2026-10-12). (KB-KYR6, live, checked 2026-10-12)
- Add-WindowsCapability -Online fails on a Windows ECS that has no NAT gateway and no EIP, because Feature-on-Demand payloads, including OpenSSH Server, are fetched over the internet. (KB-KZHC, live, checked 2026-08-24)
- The flavor generations whose console picker requires the __support_kvm_hi1822_hiovs image tag are s9, c9, m9, c4ne, c6ne, x2e, x2m and x2ne. (KB-LQ6R, live, checked 2026-07-20)
- In eu-de the ECS instance listing is GET /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/detail and returns the instances under the response key servers. Each entry carries name, id, status, flavor with vcpus and ram, addresses, metadata.vpc_id and os-extended-volumes:volumes_attached. (KB-LSFW, live, checked 2026-09-25)
- An in-place ECS resize from s3.large.2 to s3.medium.2 (stop, resize call, start) keeps the server id, the private IPs and port_ids of both NICs, the attached security group, the allowed_address_pairs source and destination check override on both ports, and the EIP binding. Nothing has to be re-applied. (KB-M2RZ, live, checked 2026-07-29)
- An ECS bound to an IAM agency gets temporary AK/SK from the link-local metadata service at GET /openstack/latest/securitykey. This is the TCP workload-identity path, the analog of an instance profile with temporary credentials, and Terraform supports it. (KB-M7O3, docs, checked 2026-07-24)
- A public Ubuntu 24.04 image ran its OTC BOOTCMD step until 637 seconds after boot on a subnet without DNS servers. After the subnet got the internal DNS server through dnsList, the user data script of the next guest from the same base ran 84 seconds after boot. (KB-MIZ3, live, checked 2026-10-01)
- On a freshly created Windows ECS in T Cloud Public (TCP) eu-de the built-in Administrator account comes up flagged password must change, even when adminPass is set in the create call. FreeRDP returns ERRCONNECT_PASSWORD_MUST_CHANGE and WinRM over NTLM returns InvalidCredentials until the password is changed interactively. (KB-MLBL, live, checked 2026-08-24)
- The eu-de service description lists the s7n general-purpose ECS family up to size 2xlarge, and lists the s9, s3 and c9 families at 8xlarge.2 with 32 vCPU and 64 GiB. (KB-MQND, contract, checked 2026-09-15)
- The os-getConsoleOutput action returns HTTP 200 with an empty output string for a guest that writes nothing to ttyS0, because only what the guest sends to the serial port is kept. The same call returns the written lines when user_data writes to /dev/ttyS0. (KB-NDWB, live, checked 2026-07-02)
- s9 flavors cap at 4 GB RAM per vCPU, so a memory-optimized m4 instance at 8 GB per vCPU matches m9 and would lose half its RAM on a move to s9. (KB-NH6R, docs, checked 2026-07-21)
- Elastic Cloud Server (ECS) provides virtual and physical compute instances (virtual servers) made of vCPUs, memory, block storage on Elastic Volume Service and a public or private operating system image, chosen from preconfigured flavors. The flavor groups are general purpose, dedicated general purpose, memory optimized, large memory, storage optimized, flexible purpose and GPU accelerated (NVIDIA T4, L4, A40, V100, A100 and H100 cards), plus physical instances; public images include Microsoft Windows Server and the Linux distributions SLES, Oracle Linux and Red Hat Enterprise Linux. The Regions and Endpoints page lists API endpoints for ECS in eu-de and eu-nl. (KB-NHAY, contract, checked 2026-10-11)
- An existing ECS resizes to a newer-generation flavor in place; the create and resize APIs ignore the flavor cond:image requirement __support_kvm_hi1822_hiovs, so an untagged image resizes fine. The tag only makes the console flavor picker list s9, and for a Windows image the console greys s9 out even with the tag. (KB-NRJ5, live, checked 2026-07-21)
- Creating a Windows ECS with adminPass in the create body works, but the injected password is flagged "user must change password at first logon", so the first RDP login forces a password change. (KB-NTZM, live, checked 2026-06-10)
- The platform accepts two ECS instances with the same name in one project, so an ECS name can match more than one instance. (KB-NYRP, live, checked 2026-09-02)
- An ECS create in eu-de through the native v2.1 API with block_device_mapping_v2 disk_bus sata on the boot volume (source image, destination volume) reaches ACTIVE, but TCP ignores the bus: the system disk is /dev/vda on virtio-blk at PCI 0000:02:01.0 and OVMF boots it as UEFI Misc Device. (KB-O442, live, checked 2026-10-07)
- A Windows ECS created with key_name receives a Cloudbase-Init generated random password that carries no must-change flag. It is fetched with the os-server-password API and decrypted locally with the private key, for example with openssl pkeyutl -decrypt. (KB-OGU3, live, checked 2026-06-11)
- The documentation describes the general-purpose ECS category on T Cloud Public (TCP) as a baseline-performance line with bursting, and the C9 family as the dedicated line for high load. (KB-ONCW, docs, checked 2026-09-15)
- ECS compute is billed per second, so a stopped instance stops accruing compute charges. EVS volumes attached to it keep billing while it is stopped, so stopping an instance reduces the compute part of its cost only. (KB-OTPK, contract, checked 2026-06-29)
- A socat proxy relaying migration traffic on an ECS failed under load with fork(): Resource temporarily unavailable, from file-descriptor and process limit exhaustion. The 'Connection reset by peer' errors seen by clients were a symptom of that exhaustion, not the cause. It was run as a systemd unit with LimitNOFILE=65536 and LimitNPROC=65536. (KB-P56B, live, checked 2026-02-04)
- On 2026-10-04 no H100 flavor could be ordered in eu-de: every p5e (H100 NVL) and p5s (H100 PCIe) size read sellout in eu-de-01, its only zone, including p5s.20xlarge.12, which read normal on 2026-09-25. (KB-PA4Y, live, checked 2026-10-04)
- The resource opentelekomcloud_ecs_instance_v1 reports the private address as nics[0].ip_address. A reference to a wrong attribute behind a splat passes terraform validate and is rejected at plan time. (KB-PDZX, live, checked 2026-09-16)
- Secure Boot is available for an ECS and is switched off by default. (KB-PJIW, docs, checked 2026-09-21)
- An ECS root image is swapped with POST /v2/{project_id}/cloudservers/{server_id}/changeos on ecs.eu-de.otc.t-systems.com, body key os-change with imageid, and the response carries job_id. The API reference lists keyname, userid, imageid and metadata in os-change but no adminpass, and the v1 form of the same path returns HTTP 404 with error code APIGW.0101. (KB-PJK5, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- An ECS of flavor s3.medium.1 with a SAS system disk boots the public image Standard_Ubuntu_22.04_latest in eu-de-01 and answers on TCP port 22 inside its VPC when a security group rule lets port 22 in from the subnet; no EIP is needed. After POST /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/delete the server read still answered 200 with status DELETED about 5 minutes later, while the server had left cloudservers/detail and its system disk was gone. (KB-PLHK, live, checked 2026-10-12)
- The changeos call expects the server in state SHUTOFF; a request against a running server carries mode withStopServer instead. (KB-PR7C, live, checked 2026-09-25)
- The image property hw_disk_bus ide puts the system disk of an ECS on c7n.large.2 in eu-de on the primary IDE channel of the PIIX3 controller, ata1 at port 0x1f0. A UEFI Ubuntu 24.04 guest booted from it as /dev/sda. (KB-PTV4, live, checked 2026-10-01)
- An ECS create in eu-de from an image with hw_machine_type q35 fails: on c7n.large.2 and c9.large.2 the job ends in Ecs.0204 with 'server is error, code is 0107012', on s9.large.2 it times out after 31 minutes, and TCP rolls the server back. (KB-PW4E, live, checked 2026-10-01)
- An ECS flavor change is POST /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/{server_id}/resize with resize.flavorRef and returns a job_id. The server has to be in SHUTOFF, stopped with POST /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/action and os-stop. The flow is stop, resize, start, with the ECS jobs endpoint polled in between. (KB-PW6W, live, checked 2026-07-20)
- U-Boot 2019.04 already contains a virtio block driver: the build options VIRTIO_PCI and VIRTIO_BLK and the command 'virtio scan' (CMD_VIRTIO, on by default when VIRTIO is set). A loader on that release can find a virtio-blk disk once these options are enabled. (KB-QAFV, docs, checked 2026-10-07)
- An empty subnet dnsList stalls cloud-init: the same image and flavor booted in about 700 seconds without resolvers and about 103 seconds with them. Check subnet DNS before blaming the flavor for slow ECS boots. (KB-QHR6, live, checked 2026-07-17)
- The NIC limit of a flavor is published as os_extra_specs quota:vif_max_num in GET /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/flavors, for example 2 for s3.large.2 with 2 vCPU and 4 GB. The same limit is mirrored in the create response as attachableQuantity.free_nic. (KB-R4JC, live, checked 2026-07-20)
- On 2026-10-04 in eu-de the one-GPU A100 flavor p3.2xlarge.8 (1 x A100 80GB PCIe) read normal in eu-de-03, its only zone, confirmed through both the ECS flavor list and the Nova os-extra_specs call, while p3.4xlarge.8, p3.8xlarge.8 and p3.16xlarge.8 read sellout. (KB-RFLA, live, checked 2026-10-04)
- root_volume.size in the ECS create body and bandwidth.size in the EIP assign body are typed Integer. A quoted value such as "40" is rejected with a type error that names the field. (KB-RIBI, live, checked 2026-08-18)
- A job_id is polled at the service that issued it, each under the same /v1/{project_id}/jobs/{job_id} shape: ECS actions such as changeos, volume attach and power operations on ecs.eu-de.otc.t-systems.com, EVS volume create and delete on evs.eu-de.otc.t-systems.com, and IMS image actions on ims.eu-de.otc.t-systems.com. (KB-RIC7, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- The documented ways to reset a Windows administrator password on an ECS are changing it inside the running OS, or detaching the system disk and attaching it to a temporary ECS. (KB-RIVY, docs, checked 2026-06-10)
- Each ECS flavor publishes a cond:image condition in os_extra_specs, and all s9 sizes list __support_kvm=true and __support_kvm_hi1822_hiovs=true. The condition refers to the image's KVM tags and virtual_env_type, not to whether the image is public or private. (KB-RK7V, live, checked 2026-07-20)
- Polling a job_id at another service's jobs endpoint returns a body holding the job_id with status null, and keeps returning that on repeat polls instead of an error. A status appears only when the jobs endpoint belongs to the API that returned the job_id. (KB-RVGO, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- Creating an ECS from an image that does not fit the flavor fails with Ecs.0005, flavor does not match image. Images named *_GPU_* fit GPU flavors only, and images with virtual_env_type Ironic fit physical flavors only. (KB-S3RC, live, checked 2026-06-11)
- The order of the nics array in the ECS create request determines the interface order inside the guest: the first entry becomes port1, the second port2. This decides the role of port1 for appliances that expect a fixed interface convention. (KB-SCHO, live, checked 2026-07-23)
- A job id returned by an EVS volume create or delete is polled on the EVS service endpoint at /v1/{project_id}/jobs/{job_id}. A job id returned by an ECS operation such as changeos, volume attach or start and stop is polled on the ECS service endpoint at the same path. (KB-SECN, live, checked 2026-09-25)
- An ECS in eu-de cannot be created with config_drive true: the native create ends in ERROR after 12 scheduling attempts with the fault 'Nova supports 24 virtio-blk disks', and the docs mark config_drive as not supported. (KB-SGDX, live, checked 2026-10-01)
- An ECS can be deleted while its elastic IP is kept, by setting delete_publicip to false in the delete request. (KB-SGOE, live, checked 2026-06-11)
- An ECS supports at most 12 NICs in total, and typical flavors cap at 2 to 4, which limits one-IP-per-pod designs to low pod density. (KB-SIZU, docs, checked 2026-06-12)
- In eu-de-01, 326 of 329 ECS flavors carry cond:operation:status = abandon while instances still run on them, and only 3 carry normal. The per-AZ condition field, not the global status field, reflects whether a flavor can be used. (KB-SPTH, live, checked 2026-08-18)
- On T Cloud Public (TCP) in eu-de, at equal vCPU and memory, the s9 general-purpose ECS family has a lower pay-as-you-go hourly rate than the s7n and s3 general-purpose families and than the dedicated c9 family. (KB-SRKV, contract, checked 2026-09-15)
- The ECS changeos operation is served at /v2/{project_id}/cloudservers/{server_id}/changeos in TCP eu-de. The same path under v1 returns HTTP 404. (KB-SW4Q, live, checked 2026-09-25)
- On a Windows guest, Cloudbase-Init sets the guest name during first boot and reboots the machine, so a deployment that sets the guest name carries one extra reboot. (KB-SXN2, live, checked 2026-09-02)
- The service description names a 99.95% availability figure for ECS, OBS and EVS, and states that in all other respects there is no minimum availability. A component counts as available (excused event) while it is reachable in at least one availability zone. (KB-T4MF, contract, checked 2026-09-02)
- An ECS of flavor x1.large.4 (4 vCPU, 8 GB) in eu-de sustained 100 to 110 MB/s for a full 400 GB anonymous download from a public HuggingFace repository, using hf download with HF_HUB_ENABLE_HF_TRANSFER=1 and 4 workers. (KB-TGQ5, live, checked 2026-05-01)
- The ECS instance UUID in the guest metadata (/openstack/latest/meta_data.json, field uuid) matches attachments[].server_id in the EVS cloudvolumes/detail response, which confirms which volume is attached to that VM. (KB-TJPS, live, checked 2026-06-08)
- Teardown of a baseline deployment follows dependency order: ECS, elastic IP, key pair, subnet, security group, VPC. (KB-U3Z7, live, checked 2026-08-18)
- First boot of a new Windows Server 2022 ECS on s9.large.2 reached RDP after 213 s, stopping it to SHUTOFF took 21 s, and starting the prepared stopped machine reached RDP after 23 s. First boot carries Cloudbase-Init hostname setting and a reboot. (KB-U7NB, live, checked 2026-09-02)
- A resize request from an s9 flavor back to s3 or s7n returns error Ecs.0804. Only s3 to s9 and s7n to s9 were observed to succeed, and cross-family or older-generation targets can be refused the same way. (KB-UCGS, live, checked 2026-07-21)
- Because cond:operation:az wins per AZ, an ECS flavor whose top-level cond:operation:status reads abandon can still be running instances in an AZ that the az parameter lists as available. (KB-UE4H, docs, checked 2026-09-02)
- After a delete, GET /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/{server_id} answered HTTP 200 with status DELETED for at least seven minutes on 2026-09-25 instead of HTTP 404, so a wait that looks for 404 alone does not see the delete. (KB-UFOT, live, checked 2026-09-25)
- Cloudbase-Init executes #ps1_sysnative user_data on T Cloud Public (TCP) Windows public images. Local users created by such a script authenticated afterwards over RDP. (KB-UKPX, live, checked 2026-08-24)
- The adminPass field of the ECS create API only satisfies platform validation. An appliance image that ignores cloud-init password injection keeps its own factory default credentials at first login. (KB-UQXM, live, checked 2026-07-23)
- TCP exposes TPM 2.0 and Secure Boot with the UEFI Windows images on c9 and m9 flavors from 8 vCPU and on the pi5e GPU flavor family. (KB-USAA, live, checked 2026-09-25)
- In eu-de on 2026-09-25 the ECS s9 family comes in 1, 2 and 4 GiB of RAM per vCPU (suffixes .1, .2 and .4, 1 to 64 vCPUs). The m9 family gives 8 GiB per vCPU, except m9.48xlarge.8 with 192 vCPUs and 1,504 GiB. (KB-V64D, live, checked 2026-09-25)
- A cold rebuild of a Linux server took 111 seconds: image 12 seconds, ECS creation 33 seconds, guest boot 66 seconds. The run used a backup taken from a stopped server. (KB-V6WG, live, checked 2026-09-02)
- A flavor that the API lists as orderable in an availability zone is a catalog entry rather than a stock guarantee. An ECS create against such a flavor can still fail with error code Ecs.0319. (KB-V7XS, live, checked 2026-08-18)
- On a Windows ECS, Cloudbase-Init takes the guest hostname from the ECS name. A restore under a different ECS name renamed the guest and added a second boot, with RDP answering after 191 s against 149 s when the ECS name matched the original. (KB-VBVZ, live, checked 2026-09-02)
- The x1, x1e and x1m ECS flavor families are priced pay-as-you-go only in section 6 of the service description and carry no reserved price rows. For a given vCPU and RAM target the cheapest pay-as-you-go flavor and the cheapest reservable one are therefore usually different flavors. (KB-VYRW, contract, checked 2026-09-25)
- An x1 ECS in eu-de got no ARP reply from c9 and s7n ECS placed in the same subnet, security group (self-rule present) and AZ, and ping reported Destination Host Unreachable, although those guests booted and reached the metadata service. (KB-W3LX, live, checked 2026-06-11)
- An ECS create call takes imageRef as an image UUID, so an image name is resolved through IMS first. Public image names encode architecture and firmware, so Standard_Ubuntu_22.04_latest and Standard_Ubuntu_24.04_amd64_uefi_latest address different images. (KB-W7Q3, live, checked 2026-08-18)
- ECS create is asynchronous and returns a job_id instead of a finished server. The job is polled for its status, and a FAIL result carries error_code and fail_reason. (KB-WE2L, live, checked 2026-08-18)
- ECS limits are read with GET /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/limits on ecs.eu-de.otc.t-systems.com. The 200 response carries an absolute object with totalInstancesUsed, maxTotalInstances, totalCoresUsed, maxTotalCores, totalRAMUsed and maxTotalRAMSize. (KB-WPJH, live, checked 2026-08-18)
- When the root volume attachment of an ECS carries delete_on_termination true, the root disk is deleted together with the server even though the delete call passes delete_volume false. (KB-WSAJ, live, checked 2026-08-18)
- An ECS on c7n.large.2 in eu-de serves the same user data at /openstack/latest/user_data and /latest/user-data and shows no CD-ROM device and no config drive. (KB-WT3L, live, checked 2026-10-01)
- Among ECS flavours only s9, c9 and m9 carry info:features = sdi together with hinic driver mode. The NIC tag they share with x2e, x2ne and c4ne and the quota:spdk_queues property present on s7n, c7n and m7n do not separate them, so the link to the SDRS restriction is correlation. (KB-X2FM, live, checked 2026-08-26)
- A resize keeps an instance in its existing availability zone, so the target flavor must have capacity in that AZ. (KB-XLKX, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- os-getConsoleOutput returns an empty output for an ECS on c9.large.2 in eu-de that boots the Meraki vMX 26.1.4 UEFI image, while the same image on c7n.large.2 returns its OVMF and U-Boot lines. Use c7n.large.2 when the serial log of this image is needed. (KB-XPGL, live, checked 2026-10-07)
- Rebuilding a Windows Server 2022 machine and renaming the guest added about 42 s: 149 s to RDP without the rename, 191 s with it. (KB-Y3BF, live, checked 2026-09-02)
- The guest console output of an ECS is read with POST /v2/{project_id}/servers/{id}/action and the body {"os-getConsoleOutput": {"length": 200}}, which returns the cloud-init lines of the boot. (KB-YHCE, live, checked 2026-08-18)
- A download from a public model hub into an ECS in eu-de held about 100 MB/s sustained over roughly 400 GB, with no account or token used on the remote side. (KB-YJF3, live, checked 2026-05-01)
- The U-Boot of the Meraki vMX 26.1.4 KVM image (2019.04-g8ff3e49edae) has no virtio command: 'virtio scan' answers Unknown command. Its bootcmd loads the system only from SCSI/AHCI: scsi scan, then ext4load from scsi 0:2 of the FIT image in the new, safe and old system folders, and bootm with the config x86_vm_config. (KB-YJUR, live, checked 2026-10-07)
- The s9 general-purpose family caps at 4 GB RAM per vCPU, so a memory-optimized m4 flavor at 8 GB per vCPU matches m9, not s9: m4.large.8 with 2 vCPU and 16 GB maps to m9.large.8, while s9.large.4 would halve the memory. (KB-YKIN, live, checked 2026-07-21)
- The Meraki vMX 26.1.4 KVM image (UEFI) on c7n.large.2 in eu-de starts its U-Boot 2019.04 EFI payload and stops at 'scanning bus for devices...', with the system disk on IDE and on virtio-blk alike. (KB-YQOS, live, checked 2026-10-01)
- TCP in eu-de refuses a SATA system disk but attaches a CD-ROM on an ATA/AHCI controller through block_device_mapping_v2: the two paths to an AHCI device behave differently. (KB-Z72S, live, checked 2026-10-02)
- In eu-de on 2026-09-25, each entry of GET /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/detail carried flavor with name, vcpus and ram, addresses with OS-EXT-IPS:type and OS-EXT-IPS:port_id, security_groups with id and name, OS-EXT-AZ:availability_zone and status with OS-EXT-STS:power_state, ACTIVE with 1 and SHUTOFF with 4. Its os-extended-volumes:volumes_attached entries hold only id, device, bootIndex and delete_on_termination, so the disk size needs GET /v1/{project_id}/cloudservers/{server_id}/block_device or the EVS API while the disk type and encryption need the EVS API. (KB-Z73M, live, checked 2026-09-25)
- For an ECS re-imaged to Windows with changeos, the console Get Password function is not available, because the instance was not originally provisioned from a Windows image and holds no key-encrypted password metadata. (KB-ZCKR, live, checked 2026-02-04)
- The Nova-compatible v2.1 surface of the ECS endpoint in eu-de accepts POST /v2.1/{project_id}/servers/{server_id}/action with the os-getConsoleOutput body and an optional length. The response returns the last lines of the instance serial port ttyS0 in the output key. (KB-ZCYK, live, checked 2026-06-11)
- In eu-de a job id polled on the endpoint of the other service returns HTTP 400 with error code Common.0011 (ECS: No Job found by jobId, EVS: query job fail) instead of a JSON body with status null (2026-09-25). (KB-ZXBG, live, checked 2026-09-25)
- os_extra_specs also reports hw:cpu_cores, hw:cpu_threads and hw:cpu_sockets as physical cores, threads per core and sockets. An 8 vCPU flavor of the *.2xlarge.2 size resolves to 4 cores by 2 threads on 1 socket, one NUMA node. (KB-ZYK5, live, checked 2026-07-19)
- cloud-init skips a user_data script whose #! line is indented. The guest log then shows cc_scripts_user.py[WARNING]: Failed to run module scripts-user and a failed cloud-final.service, while the ECS still reaches status ACTIVE with its security group and EIP bound. (KB-ZYW7, live, checked 2026-08-18)

## obs (150)

- OBS SDK guides are published for python, java, go, nodejs, dotnet and c. Community OBS SDK sources for python and java are published under the opentelekomcloud-community organisation. (KB-24VK, docs, checked 2026-06-19)
- The OBS user guide limits a user-defined domain name bound to a bucket to HTTP requests instead of HTTPS, and the domain name points by a CNAME record to the bucket domain name, so a DNS lookup of the own domain returns the OBS bucket host. (KB-252Q, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- In an OBS bucket policy the principal is written as domain/{domain_id}:user/{user_id} with a colon between the two parts; a slash returns HTTP 400 MalformedPolicy. Actions use plain S3 names such as ListBucket, GetObject and PutObject without an obs: prefix, and Resource is the bucket name or bucket/prefix/*. (KB-2A76, live, checked 2026-06-17)
- DLI does not create Iceberg tables: on 2026-09-25 the DLI SQL check refused CREATE TABLE ... USING ICEBERG with DLI.0007 and STORED BY with a Hive storage handler with DLI.0005. SQL over Iceberg on TCP goes through MRS HetuEngine or Impala instead. (KB-2MWY, live, checked 2026-09-25)
- obsutil 5.7.9 stores the access key and secret key in its configuration file in encrypted form, 44 and 64 characters instead of 20 and 40, so a prefix comparison against the plain key reports a mismatch even for the correct key. (KB-2QVO, docs, checked 2026-08-25)
- A bucket policy that denies every action on the bucket and its objects to NotPrincipal one IAM user, plus an Allow for that user, leaves that user able to write, read and delete objects with an access key, while anonymous list, read and write requests get 403 AccessDenied. (KB-2SQ3, live, checked 2026-09-23)
- When OBS receives a request to configure bucket event notification, it checks that the SMN topic exists and is authorized to OBS and then sends a test notification to the topic subscribers. The OBS troubleshooting page gives the console message "OBS is not authorized to use this topic" for a topic without that authorization and the fix: select OBS under "Services that can publish messages to this topic" in the SMN topic policy. (KB-2T4U, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- A bucket's default storage class applies to new uploads that omit a storage class. Objects already stored keep the class they were written with. (KB-34WC, docs, checked 2026-02-04)
- The SigV4 signing region is not enforced for OBS listing: a scope signed for us-east-1 returned HTTP 200 on ListBuckets and on the path-style bucket listing in eu-de. Write requests were not tested. (KB-37YE, live, checked 2026-09-15)
- OBS Warm has a minimum storage period of 30 days and OBS Cold of 90 days. These periods and the early-deletion mechanics stand in the charging-method section of the service description, not in the price table. (KB-3CKT, contract, checked 2026-08-27)
- A signed GET on the OBS service endpoint root, addressing no bucket, returns an XML ListAllMyBucketsResult document listing the buckets of the account in eu-de. (KB-3DTX, live, checked 2026-07-09)
- In SigV4 against OBS a value-less subresource is canonicalised with a trailing equals sign, as location=. Signing plain location returns 403 SignatureDoesNotMatch, which reads like a key or permission problem. (KB-3FLB, live, checked 2026-09-15)
- Batch object deletion uses POST /?delete with an XML Delete body of at most 1000 keys and requires a Content-MD5 header, otherwise OBS rejects the request. (KB-3GDY, live, checked 2026-07-09)
- obsutil config with the -config option only updates a configuration file that already exists. Pointed at a path with no file at it, the command returns "no such file or directory", so an existing config has to be copied to that path first. (KB-3V6A, live, checked 2026-08-25)
- Deleting an OBS bucket returns HTTP 409 BucketNotEmpty while an incomplete multipart upload still exists in it, even when every object key has been removed. Aborting the pending uploads clears the condition. (KB-3WA4, live, checked 2026-07-09)
- PUT /{bucket}/?customdomain={domain} returns HTTP 200 and is mandatory for a custom domain on OBS. Before the binding, requests carrying the custom Host header got 404 although the bucket name equalled that host, so the service does no implicit host matching. (KB-3ZOF, live, checked 2026-07-17)
- An OBS lifecycle transition is billed at the request rate of the destination storage class, not the source class. At petabyte scale with objects around 512 KiB the one-off request charge of a transition is a headline number, not a rounding error. (KB-4E6N, docs, checked 2026-09-02)
- PUT /{bucket}/?website with an IndexDocument XML body returns HTTP 200, and PUT /{bucket}/?policy with a public-read GetObject policy returns HTTP 204 on OBS in eu-de. (KB-4G6T, live, checked 2026-07-17)
- Temporary OBS access keys carry a security token and are valid from 15 minutes up to 24 hours. The SDK documentation names them as the client-side alternative to permanent keys. (KB-4IUG, docs, checked 2026-06-19)
- Writing to OBS with default boto3 1.43.57 stores the aws-chunked trailer framing as the object body: put_object of a 9-byte body returns success with an ETag, and head_object then reports 50 bytes. botocore 1.36 and later switch flexible checksums on by default with request_checksum_calculation = when_supported. (KB-4LB5, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- On TCP an OBS object-arrival trigger for a function is created with POST /v2/{project_id}/fgs/triggers/{function_urn}, trigger_type_code OBS, which returned 201 with trigger_status ACTIVE. Event names in event_data need the s3: prefix, for example s3:ObjectCreated:*. (KB-4M2N, live, checked 2026-07-29)
- Setting the Config/RMS tracker configuration with an OBS bucket as channel returned HTTP 403 with error code RMS.00010006. The same call with an SMN topic as channel succeeded on the first attempt. (KB-53ET, live, checked 2026-07-17)
- In a 27-operation S3 compatibility matrix against OBS, 25 operations passed, among them ranged GET, prefix and delimiter listing, MaxKeys with ContinuationToken, StartAfter, copy, conditional GET, batch delete, object tagging, versioning, lifecycle, presigned URLs and bucket default encryption. PASS records that the API accepted the call, since the run used the corrupting checksum default. (KB-5CA3, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- obsutil copies between two OBS buckets that belong to different tenants by holding one credential set as the default profile and the other as the CRR profile. The data streams through the client host and uses its CPU, memory and bandwidth, unlike a server-side copy inside one tenant. (KB-5MXI, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- obsutil ls reports a File number capped at 1000 unless the -limit parameter is raised. Object counts of a source and a destination prefix compared at the default therefore differ even when both sides hold the same objects. (KB-5ZGT, live, checked 2026-06-17)
- An OBS parallel file system takes SSE-KMS only as a bucket default combining BucketKeyEnabled true with an explicit KMSMasterKeyID, all together. Objects then report ServerSideEncryption aws:kms with no per-request header. (KB-64ON, live, checked 2026-07-29)
- TCP exposes no array-level immutable snapshot storage to tenants. Tenant-facing WORM comes from OBS Object Lock, which covers objects only, and from CBR backup locking, which keeps automatic backups in server, disk and SFS Turbo vaults undeletable during their retention period while manual backups stay deletable (checked 2026-09-25). (KB-66D6, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- A presigned OBS URL is bound to its object key, its HTTP method and its expiry. Reuse against another key, use after the Expires value, and a DELETE sent to a URL presigned for PUT each return HTTP 403. (KB-6AP2, live, checked 2026-06-26)
- obsutil stores the multipart uploadId in a checkpoint file under its local `.obsutil_checkpoint/upload` directory and reuses that uploadId when the same copy is retried. (KB-6IAO, live, checked 2026-05-01)
- An OBS presigned URL in the OBS signature form carries AccessKeyId, Expires (a Unix timestamp) and a Signature over the HTTP method, Content-MD5, Content-Type, Expires, the x-obs-* headers and the bucket and object key with sub-resources such as response-content-type, so it is bound to one method and one object key while the scheme stays outside the signed string. It is signed with an AK/SK pair or with a temporary AK/SK pair plus x-obs-security-token. (KB-6K64, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- In the service description §6.4.1 OBS Standard storage is charged per GiB per month in volume tiers with the first 5 GiB free, and outbound traffic is a separate item with its own tiers. (KB-6LWE, contract, checked 2026-09-25)
- Addressing an OBS bucket whose name contains dots in virtual-host style fails TLS SNI validation in eu-de. (KB-737E, live, checked 2026-09-16)
- SSE-OBS is refused on an OBS parallel file system, both as a bucket default and as a per-object header. (KB-7BI4, live, checked 2026-07-29)
- Iceberg rewrite_data_files compacts only within a partition, so it leaves a table with one file per partition as it is. Measured on OBS with 1,152,000 rows: a 6-level spec wrote 19,200 files of 2.7 KB in 140 s and compaction rewrote 0 files; a facility, hours(ts) spec wrote 96 files in 3 s. (KB-7KLV, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- OBS in eu-de does not accept the SDK-HMAC-SHA256 signature of the API gateway: a request signed that way gets HTTP 400 InvalidArgument 'Authorization header is invalid -- one and only one ' ' (space) required'. OBS needs its own signature. (KB-A3WA, live, checked 2026-10-02)
- OBS is a global service: its permissions are assigned under Global services and a system policy granted there covers every bucket of the account. To limit a user group to specific buckets, grant it a custom policy whose resources name those buckets or put the buckets in an enterprise project and grant OBS on that project only (IAM lists OBS as supporting enterprise project authorization). In both cases leave no system policy that covers all buckets on the group, such as Tenant Administrator, Tenant Guest or OBS Operator. (KB-AGQK, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- Each PUT of an OBS bucket policy replaces the whole policy, so every statement has to be sent again. Several principals mean several statements, and one principal can hold read on the whole bucket plus write on a single prefix. (KB-AOKD, live, checked 2026-06-17)
- For an MRS cluster with its Hive warehouse on OBS, set mrs_ecs_default_agency to MRS_ECS_DEFAULT_AGENCY in the create-cluster body and use an OBS parallel file system with the obs:// scheme. Nodes then get temporary credentials from the metadata service, so Spark and the Hive Metastore need no AK/SK in any config file. (KB-AU3G, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- The OBS user guide in the docs mirror of 2026-09-25 marks SMN-enabled event notifications as about to be taken offline: existing rules can still be viewed or deleted, but they cannot be modified and no new rules can be added. (KB-BICR, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- The Config resource recorder is configured with PUT /v1/resource-manager/domains/{domain_id}/tracker-config. An SMN channel was accepted with HTTP 200, while an OBS channel returned 403 RMS.00010006 about the agency missing permission on the bucket, even with OBS OperateAccess and Administrator granted and a bucket policy in place. (KB-BKP3, live, checked 2026-07-17)
- FunctionGraph triggers are created with POST /v2/{project_id}/fgs/triggers/{urn} and listed with GET on the same path. A TIMER trigger takes event_data with schedule_type "Rate" and a schedule such as 5m. An OBS trigger takes a bucket, event types such as s3:ObjectCreated:Put, plus prefix and suffix. (KB-BMHI, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- For an OBS static website on a custom domain the bucket name must equal the domain exactly. Such a bucket is managed through path-style URLs of the form https://obs.eu-de.otc.t-systems.com/{bucket}/..., because the dots in the name break the wildcard TLS certificate used for virtual-host style addressing. (KB-BPOF, live, checked 2026-07-17)
- Three concurrent writers with four append rounds each on one Iceberg table on OBS kept all 1,200 rows and 12 snapshots with both the Hive Metastore catalog and the Hadoop catalog. Three simultaneous row-level UPDATEs on one partition all succeeded. (KB-BRPI, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- With SSE-KMS the ETag OBS returns for an object differs from the plaintext MD5 and changes between attempts, so a client verifying by MD5 reports a hash mismatch although the stored data is correct. Size comparison still matches. (KB-BTOI, live, checked 2026-06-15)
- A multipart upload to OBS under default boto3 fails with XAmzContentSHA256Mismatch, while the single-object path returns success and stores the framed body. (KB-BUEP, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- An OBS bucket used as an S3 remote-state backend is configured with the backend skip_* flags, skip_s3_checksum among them. (KB-CCSA, live, checked 2026-09-17)
- In an OBS bucket policy the principal reads domain/{domain_id}:user/{user_id}, with a colon between the domain part and the user part. A slash in that position returns 400 MalformedPolicy with "invalid principal". (KB-CJ4H, live, checked 2026-06-17)
- A presigned OBS URL is issued on the bucket host, which in eu-de is the bucket name in front of obs.eu-de.otc.t-systems.com, with AccessKeyId, Expires and Signature as query parameters, so a user who gets the link sees the platform domain. (KB-CQHL, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- The S3-compatible OBS interface does not process If-None-Match on PUT. A presigned PUT sent with If-None-Match: * overwrote the existing object and answered HTTP 200 instead of 412, so a conditional header gives no create-only upload on that interface. (KB-D5FT, live, checked 2026-06-26)
- In a recursive `obsutil cp` upload to OBS, a `.cache` directory and its files were uploaded while an `-exclude` pattern with an absolute path ending in `/.cache/*` was set. (KB-DDGQ, live, checked 2026-05-01)
- An OBS bucket policy is written with a PUT on the bucket and the ?policy sub-resource, signed V2. The string to sign is "PUT\n\n\n{rfc1123 gmt date}\n/{bucket}/?policy" and the header form is Authorization: OBS {access_key}:{base64 hmac_sha1 signature}. (KB-DK67, live, checked 2026-06-17)
- OBS answers standard S3 clients at https://obs.eu-de.otc.t-systems.com with region eu-de, signature version 4 and virtual-hosted addressing. Listing, download and upload were verified this way with boto3. (KB-DR24, live, checked 2026-06-19)
- Default server-side encryption on an OBS bucket is set with a PUT on the bucket and the ?encryption sub-resource, carrying a ServerSideEncryptionConfiguration whose SSEAlgorithm is kms. Objects written afterwards answer HEAD with the header x-obs-server-side-encryption: kms. (KB-DVX5, live, checked 2026-06-15)
- After `obsutil abort obs://<bucket> -r -f`, a retry that still finds the old checkpoint file fails with 404 NoSuchUpload, because the uploadId was invalidated server-side. Deleting the checkpoint file makes the retry start a fresh multipart upload with a new uploadId. (KB-E3O4, live, checked 2026-05-01)
- Against OBS a boto3 client with signature_version s3v4 handles objects and the bucket policy alike: PUT, GET and DELETE of a bucket policy worked on 2026-09-25, and path-style addressing worked as well as virtual. The S3 API expects the policy in S3 syntax (Principal AWS arn:aws:iam::<domain>:root, Action s3:GetObject, Resource arn:aws:s3:::<bucket>/<key>); a policy in the native OBS syntax is answered with HTTP 400 MalformedPolicy. (KB-E73J, live, checked 2026-09-25)
- OBS expects its own V2 signing scheme. A request to the OBS endpoint signed with the V4 algorithm returns 400 even when the access key is valid, which reads like a broken key. (KB-EA2F, live, checked 2026-08-24)
- With the S3 backend lockfile option enabled against OBS, a concurrent-apply race was reproduced: two applies ran against the same state object and neither was blocked. (KB-EJUP, live, checked 2026-07-17)
- On a normal OBS bucket Object Lock is enforced: with versioning enabled and put_object_lock_configuration set to COMPLIANCE, a versioned delete_object returns AccessDenied: The object you specified is immutable. (KB-ETIT, live, checked 2026-07-29)
- OBS works as an Iceberg warehouse for Spark through the hadoop-aws S3A connector when path-style access is enabled and the endpoint is set to the regional OBS endpoint obs.eu-de.otc.t-systems.com with an AK/SK credentials provider. (KB-EYIH, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- An OBS bucket delete can return 409 BucketNotEmpty while ListObjects shows zero objects, because incomplete multipart uploads still occupy the bucket. The pending uploads are listed by GET /?uploads and aborted with DELETE /{key}?uploadId=..., after which the bucket delete succeeds. (KB-F53U, live, checked 2026-07-09)
- A bucket-level default SSE-KMS policy on OBS encrypts every object uploaded after the policy is set, so objects written afterwards are already encrypted at rest. (KB-F7NF, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- In the OBS docs the native API sets the default bucket storage class with ?storageClass and a StorageClass body (STANDARD, WARM, COLD), while the S3-compatible API uses ?storagePolicy with StoragePolicy and DefaultStorageClass (STANDARD, STANDARD_IA, GLACIER). Both V2 signature specs list storagePolicy among the sub-resources that go into the string to sign. (KB-FB3W, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- An OBS bucket policy is set with a PUT to the bucket root carrying the ?policy sub-resource, which answers HTTP 204 on success. (KB-FLQO, live, checked 2026-06-17)
- The DLI docs disagree on the partition limit of OBS tables: the SQL reference pages on creating OBS tables give 200,000 partitions per table for both DataSource and Hive syntax, while the developer guide still gives 7,000 for DataSource and 100,000 for Hive syntax. Two other SQL reference pages give 100,000 (INSERT) and 7,000 (OVER clause) partitions per table. (KB-FQLZ, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- OBS Object Lock (WORM) gives true write-once immutability on TCP, but it covers objects only; block volumes, file shares and snapshots are outside its scope. (KB-GFBA, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- An OBS-triggered FunctionGraph handler receives an event with a Records array, where the object key sits under Records[].obs.object.key. (KB-GJHC, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- An IAM custom fine-grained policy granting obs:bucket:ListBucket and obs:object:GetObject on one bucket, assigned through a group at domain level, still gave HTTP 403 for OBS access with that user's access key more than 20 minutes after assignment. An OBS bucket policy granting the same access took effect within seconds. (KB-GPNI, live, checked 2026-06-17)
- S3 Select against OBS returns MethodNotAllowed. awswrangler is one client that issues S3 Select calls. (KB-GRFT, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- The custom domain is pointed at the website endpoint with an unproxied CNAME to {bucket}.obs-website.eu-de.otc.t-systems.com. The site answered over plain HTTP, OBS served no certificate for the custom domain. (KB-GRRS, live, checked 2026-07-17)
- An OBS object moves to the Warm storage class at the earliest 30 days after its last update, so the first 30 days are billed at Standard whatever the lifecycle policy says. (KB-GV7Z, docs, checked 2026-09-02)
- A copy between two OBS buckets in the same region is executed inside OBS. The client reports a transferred size of 0B while thousands of objects are copied, and no object data passes through the client network. (KB-GXBI, live, checked 2026-06-17)
- A verified S3-compatible remote state backend against the OBS endpoint in eu-de has credential validation, region validation, the account-id request, the metadata API check and the S3 checksum all skipped. (KB-GY5W, live, checked 2026-07-17)
- AWS DataSync in Enhanced mode moves data from S3 to third-party object storage without an agent; the agent and the rule that both locations sit in the agent Region apply to Basic mode only. An Enhanced task fails against a target that does not support object tagging unless the tag option is set. (KB-HDCR, docs, checked 2026-09-01)
- Setting request_checksum_calculation to when_required stops the OBS body corruption. It works as the environment variable AWS_REQUEST_CHECKSUM_CALCULATION, as a key in a botocore Config object, or as the same key in the AWS CLI config file. obsutil is unaffected and wrote the 9-byte body correctly in the same run. (KB-HEFL, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- WORM retention can be switched on for an OBS bucket that already exists, verified by a live call. The user manual states the opposite and is wrong on this point. (KB-HHVN, live, checked 2026-09-01)
- An OBS multi-object delete request requires the Content-MD5 header, holding the base64 encoding of the raw MD5 digest of the XML request body. (KB-HIW3, live, checked 2026-07-09)
- The OBS website endpoint name resolves differently depending on the resolver: from inside a VPC it returns an internal platform service address, from the internet a public address, for the same name. (KB-HJVH, live, checked 2026-07-17)
- The Swift-compatible object interface of OBS honours If-None-Match: * on upload and answers HTTP 412 when the object already exists. It is a different API surface from the S3-compatible one used for S3-style presigned URLs. (KB-HMN7, docs, checked 2026-06-26)
- The DLI.0007 error for CREATE TABLE ... USING on OBS tables lists PARQUET, ORC, JSON, CSV, HUDI and DELTA as supported formats, while the DLI documentation lists CSV, Parquet, ORC, JSON and Avro. Creating a HUDI or DELTA table itself is untested. (KB-I4YA, live, checked 2026-09-25)
- OpenTofu 1.12.6 reads a state file written by Terraform 1.16 (state format 4) without error. (KB-I6S4, live, checked 2026-09-17)
- When a bucket policy lists domain/{domain_id}:root in NotPrincipal, OBS returns that entry as domain/{domain_id} on a GET of the policy. (KB-IF4S, live, checked 2026-09-23)
- An S3 client that sends an implicit CreateBucket before uploading receives InvalidRequest with the message that header x-amz-bucket-object-lock-enabled must be 'true'. Writing into an already existing bucket succeeds. (KB-ILRB, live, checked 2026-06-15)
- T Cloud Public (TCP) offers object storage for archive use in Object Storage Service (OBS): the Cold storage class is meant for data accessed about once a year, such as archives and long-term backups, lifecycle rules transition objects to the Warm or Cold class or delete them, and WORM retention in compliance mode keeps object versions from being overwritten or deleted by anyone for a set period. Cold objects are restored before they are read (KB-TA3K). (KB-JMBA, docs, checked 2026-10-11)
- Versioning is refused on an OBS parallel file system, and Object Lock requires versioning first, so WORM retention does not run on a parallel file system. (KB-JP4L, live, checked 2026-07-29)
- Restoring a Cold (GLACIER) OBS object with the Standard retrieval tier took about one to five hours in the observed runs. (KB-JRLG, live, checked 2026-02-04)
- AWS SDKs running the 2025 default checksum mode upload objects to OBS with aws-chunked framing written inside the object body, and the upload reports success. (KB-K4AE, live, checked 2026-09-15)
- Object Storage Service (OBS) is a cloud-native object storage service in which the customer creates buckets and stores and manages objects in them, through the console, the API or the S3 protocol. It is offered in the storage classes Standard, Warm and Cold, and it stores data redundantly across three availability zones. The Regions and Endpoints page lists API endpoints for OBS in eu-de and eu-nl. (KB-KD7R, contract, checked 2026-10-11)
- An account including all its IAM users can create at most 100 buckets and parallel file systems together. By default there is no limit on the capacity or on the number of objects in a bucket. (KB-KUBY, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- The OBS API reference writes bucket notification events without a prefix, ObjectCreated:Put, Post, Copy, CompleteMultipartUpload or ObjectCreated:*, and ObjectRemoved:Delete, DeleteMarkerCreated or ObjectRemoved:*, and puts only TopicConfiguration elements into NotificationConfiguration. The sample of the S3-compatible PUT Bucket Notification page writes s3:ObjectCreated:* instead. (KB-LIWX, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- In an OBS bucket notification configuration only TopicConfiguration is stored. A body with QueueConfiguration returns HTTP 200 while the configuration is not kept, and the notification does not fire. (KB-LP7I, live, checked 2026-08-27)
- obsutil reads credentials from its own configuration file, so after an access key rotation every call returns HTTP 403 with error code InvalidAccessKeyId until obsutil config is run again with the new key. (KB-M3YL, live, checked 2026-08-25)
- customdomain is a signed OBS subresource: a request that omits it from the string to sign is rejected as a signature mismatch. (KB-MKEY, live, checked 2026-07-17)
- A completed obsutil rm prints output that omits the word succeed, so a success check grepping for that word reports a failure on a delete that worked. Listing the target confirms the delete. (KB-ML34, live, checked 2026-08-25)
- The File number that obsutil ls prints is capped by its default -limit of 1000, so for a prefix holding more objects the printed count is lower than the real one. (KB-NJJU, live, checked 2026-06-17)
- Completing an OBS multipart upload returns 400 Bad Request when the merged object would exceed 48.8 TB or when a part other than the last one is smaller than 100 KB. (KB-NRLY, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- A new IAM user whose group held the system role OBS Administrator of type AX at domain level still got 403 on HEAD bucket and on ListBuckets for 30 minutes after the grant. (KB-NRZ6, live, checked 2026-09-23)
- OBS in eu-de accepts object writes signed with the OBS-specific (S3-style) signature, while the same writes signed over the API gateway signature path (SDK-HMAC-SHA256) still fail. (KB-OL6L, live, checked 2026-10-03)
- OBS answers S3 Signature V4 requests from third-party clients over HTTPS at the regional endpoint, signing region eu-de. A hand-rolled SigV4 client got HTTP 200 on ListBuckets, on path-style and virtual-hosted bucket listings, and on the ?location= subresource, which returned eu-de. (KB-ORXG, live, checked 2026-09-15)
- An IAM user's console session counts as the principal domain/{domain_id}:user/{user_id} of a bucket policy: uploading an object through the console worked on a bucket whose policy denies every action to NotPrincipal that one user and allows that user. (KB-OUOG, live, checked 2026-09-23)
- An OBS bucket is created with a PUT to the bucket root carrying a CreateBucketConfiguration body with Location eu-de and Content-Type application/xml, which answers HTTP 200. A DELETE to the same bucket root after all objects are removed answers HTTP 204. (KB-OWQ7, live, checked 2026-06-12)
- The S3-compatible bucket notification call against OBS in eu-de returned InvalidArgument, Unable to validate the following destination configurations, when the destination was an SMN topic urn. The SMN topic itself had been created with 201 in the same run. (KB-OXT6, live, checked 2026-07-29)
- An OBS bucket listing returns a Size element per object together with IsTruncated. The next page is requested with max-keys and marker set to the last Key of the previous page, so total bucket size can be summed from the listings. (KB-OYZJ, live, checked 2026-09-16)
- OBS does not accept requests signed with the API gateway scheme SDK-HMAC-SHA256: a bucket listing signed that way answered HTTP 400 InvalidArgument, 'Authorization header is invalid -- one and only one ' ' (space) required'. OBS takes S3-style signatures (SigV2 or SigV4) or its own OBS scheme only. (KB-PHQ5, live, checked 2026-10-05)
- During an OBS delta sync a NoSuchKey error appears for an object that was deleted between the listing call and the copy call. It reflects a source-side delete, not a failed copy. (KB-PQYE, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- OBS objects in the GLACIER storage class need a restore to STANDARD before a copy or an encryption run can read them. (KB-PWBT, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- A single OBS object can be up to 48.8 TB when it is uploaded with OBS Browser+, the API or an SDK. Through the API this size needs multipart upload because a single PUT or POST upload takes at most 5 GB. (KB-PWSD, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- Uploading a large directory to OBS with obsutil from a 4 vCPU ECS in eu-de lost 3 of 81 parts to TCP drops at parallelism 16. The same upload at parallelism 8 finished with no failed parts. (KB-QDQE, live, checked 2026-05-01)
- A multipart upload to OBS is limited to 10000 parts of 100 KB to 5 GB each (only the last part may be smaller), so the part size must be at least the object size divided by 10000. A 142 GiB object at 128 MiB parts uses 1136 parts. (KB-QLRN, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- OBS object-lock retention covers only objects uploaded after the policy is set, and uploading the same key again creates a new current version that sits outside the protection. (KB-R7FC, docs, checked 2026-09-01)
- A bucket-level OBS request must go to the endpoint of the bucket's own region: a bucket created in eu-de returned 404 NoSuchBucket through the eu-nl endpoint. ListBuckets answered through either regional endpoint. (KB-RLOL, live, checked 2026-09-15)
- On TCP, OBS parallel file systems do not support server-side encryption: the PFS feature guide (release 2026-06-23) lists it as a constraint and marks the bucket encryption APIs as not compatible. For SSE-KMS, OBS bucket default encryption takes SSEAlgorithm kms with an optional KMSMasterKeyID and ProjectID and has no BucketKeyEnabled setting. (KB-RM33, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- OBS V2 signing includes the query sub-resource in the canonical resource string. A request to GET /?storageinfo signed without it returns HTTP 403 SignatureDoesNotMatch in both path and virtual-host style, while a plain listing of the same bucket with the same key succeeds. (KB-RT73, live, checked 2026-09-16)
- An upload of about 400 GB spread over 81 objects, sent from an ECS to an OBS bucket in the same region, took roughly 65 minutes. (KB-S4QM, live, checked 2026-05-01)
- OBS bucket notification event names are written without the s3: prefix. ObjectCreated:* is the accepted form. (KB-SAM5, live, checked 2026-08-27)
- Copying 81 files of about 5 GB each to OBS with `obsutil cp -r -p 16` from a 4 vCPU host failed on 3 files with `write tcp: use of closed network connection` and exit code 8. The targeted retries with `-p 8` finished without a failure. (KB-SD7O, live, checked 2026-05-01)
- An OBS bucket without a bucket policy is closed to other accounts by its private ACL only. Inside the owning account every IAM user whose IAM permissions allow OBS reaches it, because ACLs do not control the IAM users of that account. Only an explicit Deny in a bucket policy overrides such an IAM allow. (KB-SPIK, docs, checked 2026-09-23)
- A WORM-protected OBS object can have its storage class changed by a lifecycle rule while the retention lock still blocks deletion of the object. (KB-SQDE, docs, checked 2026-09-02)
- OBS Cold storage restore has three documented speeds: Expedited 1 to 5 minutes, Standard 3 to 5 hours and Bulk 5 to 12 hours. (KB-SSWP, docs, checked 2026-09-02)
- The system-managed obs/default key, like evs/default, cannot be granted to another tenant. Either the data is decrypted first or a custom CMK is used. (KB-T4BR, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- An OBS object in the Cold (GLACIER) class has to be restored to STANDARD before it can be read, copied or re-encrypted. The S3-compatible restore request takes a number of days and a retrieval tier; a HEAD on the object reports Restore ongoing-request="false" once the copy is ready. (KB-TA3K, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- On OBS, WORM retention on a bucket and cross-region replication of that bucket are mutually exclusive, per the OBS documentation. (KB-TA6I, docs, checked 2026-09-01)
- The default storage class of an OBS bucket is set with PUT on the bucket URL with the ?storageClass sub-resource and an XML StorageClass body naming STANDARD, WARM or COLD, and read back with GET ?storageClass. The IAM action is obs:bucket:PutBucketStoragePolicy. (KB-TD5E, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- Object Storage is billed with a cumulative graduated price: the price scales are filled one after another and each part of the used quantity is billed at its own scale. Taking the single tier a volume lands in and multiplying the whole volume by that rate over-states the bill. (KB-TPA7, contract, checked 2026-08-27)
- An IAM user reaches one OBS state bucket through a bucket policy naming the principal as domain/<domain_id>:user/<user_id>, with no OBS role assigned at all. A second bucket in the same account stayed denied for that user. (KB-TQIX, live, checked 2026-09-17)
- OBS Bucket is a resource type supported by enterprise projects. Buckets can be placed in an enterprise project and a user group authorized with an OBS role scoped to that enterprise project. (KB-UGXP, docs, checked 2026-07-09)
- OBS ignores the conditional If-None-Match header on PUT through its S3-compatible API, so a write that should fail when the object already exists overwrites it instead. (KB-UHIV, live, checked 2026-07-17)
- Throughput between an ECS and OBS is bound by the EVS volume when another heavy job uses the same disk: a copy sharing one volume ran at 0.37 MiB/s, while the same OBS path on an idle instance ran at about 237 MiB/s. More EIP bandwidth does not change this. (KB-UKAC, live, checked 2026-06-19)
- OBS accepts the native V2 signature for object keys with spaces, brackets and umlauts when the key is percent-encoded the same way in the request path and in the canonical resource of the string to sign. (KB-UPS3, live, checked 2026-09-24)
- To pin a specific custom CMK as the bucket default encryption on OBS, the PUT ?encryption body carries KMSMasterKeyID inside ApplyServerSideEncryptionByDefault next to SSEAlgorithm kms. Without that element the default key obs/default is used, and the documented success response is HTTP 200. (KB-VOUX, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
