- OBS objects in the Cold storage class have to be restored before their content can be read. A bucket wide SSE-KMS re-encryption pass therefore triggers a restore for every cold object before it can rewrite it. (KB-VP4L, live, checked 2026-02-04)
- An OBS bucket policy cannot protect itself inside its own account. An IAM user with object-storage rights in the owning account, not named in the policy, got 403 on HEAD, list, read, write, delete and ACL read under a Deny for every action to NotPrincipal one user, yet read the policy (200), replaced it (204) and deleted it (204), even when a second Deny named GetBucketPolicy, PutBucketPolicy and DeleteBucketPolicy. (KB-VSBB, live, checked 2026-09-23)
- A client-side CRR copy reads objects in the STANDARD storage class. Objects in the Cold storage class have to be restored before a copy can read them. (KB-VV4F, live, checked 2026-02-04)
- The OBS console uploads up to 100 files per batch with at most 5 GB in total and a single file there is also capped at 5 GB. For larger files the docs point to OBS Browser+ or multipart upload through the SDKs and APIs. (KB-W2R3, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- Actions in an OBS bucket policy use plain S3 names without a service prefix, such as ListBucket, GetObject and PutObject. The resource is the bucket name for bucket-level actions and bucket/* for objects, so a write can be narrowed to bucket/prefix/*. (KB-W3AJ, live, checked 2026-06-17)
- The Content-Type header is part of the OBS V2 string to sign. A PUT whose Content-Type is added to the header set after the signature is computed returns HTTP 403, because the header travels with the request but is outside the signature. (KB-WDDP, live, checked 2026-06-10)
- SSE-S3 (AES256) on OBS maps to SSE-OBS rather than to SSE-KMS, confirmed per object and as a bucket default on a standard bucket. (KB-WGCN, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- An OBS parallel file system rejects SSE-OBS and accepts SSE-KMS when the request supplies a bucket key and an explicit key id. (KB-WI66, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- The OBS documentation asks for virtual-hosted-style requests when a request carries a bucket name, and states that virtual-hosted HTTPS fails certificate verification for bucket names containing a dot. Path-style requests still answered HTTP 200 in a live eu-de test. (KB-WQBX, docs, checked 2026-09-15)
- Bucket default encryption on an OBS parallel file system applies only to objects written after it is set. Data already written stays unencrypted, and a freshly created parallel file system picks up the default cleanly. (KB-WS7V, live, checked 2026-07-29)
- An OBS bucket policy in eu-de that denies ListBucket to every principal (Principal ID "*") also refuses the owning account: its ListBucket answers 403 AccessDenied. The owner can still delete the bucket (204). (KB-WXLI, live, checked 2026-10-12)
- If an SMN topic used for OBS event notifications is deleted or loses its authorization for OBS, the OBS user guide warns that its subscribers may stop getting messages and that the event notification rules tied to the topic may be cleared automatically. (KB-WYFA, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- A presigned OBS GET URL gives time-limited read access to one object without handing over the secret key, while the URL itself carries the creator's access key ID, the Expires time and the signature. The link stops working when the expiry passes or when the object is deleted. (KB-WZOI, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- On TCP in eu-de an OBS multi-object delete request (POST /?delete) accepts at most 1000 object keys. Emptying a bucket with more objects takes one request per batch of that size. (KB-XUBF, live, checked 2026-07-09)
- PUT /?notification on a bucket answers 400 InvalidArgument for an unsupported event, a wrong or unknown topic URN, a topic in another region than the bucket or overlapping filter rules, and 403 AccessDenied when the caller is neither the bucket owner nor granted PutBucketNotification. (KB-XUL7, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- OBS can be reached from on-premises without an EIP through a Direct Connect virtual gateway into a VPC that holds a VPC Endpoint for OBS, with the documented OBS service CIDR routed to the Direct Connect gateway. Traffic then stays off the internet gateway. (KB-XUWV, docs, checked 2026-09-02)
- An OBS presigned URL uses V2 query-string auth with the parameters AccessKeyId, Expires and Signature, signing method, expiry and resource path with HMAC-SHA1. The resource in the string to sign must carry the URL-encoded object key; the raw key returns HTTP 403 for keys containing spaces. (KB-XZYG, live, checked 2026-06-11)
- A PUT on the target key with the header x-obs-copy-source: /{bucket}/{percent-encoded source key} copies an object inside the bucket without the data leaving the object storage; the header is signed as an x-obs header in the V2 string to sign. (KB-YTYI, live, checked 2026-09-24)
- While a restored OBS Cold object is available, the object is billed twice: once as the Cold object and once as the restored copy, which appears as "OBS Cold Object Storage Buffer". (KB-Z2QP, docs, checked 2026-09-02)
- The OBS user guides make a KMS permission a prerequisite for SSE-KMS uploads and downloads: KMS Administrator in the region, or a custom policy allowing kms:dek:crypto, kms:dek:create, kms:cmk:get, kms:cmk:list, kms:cmk:generate and kms:cmk:crypto. The OBS Browser guide sets this prerequisite in a flow that encrypts with the default key obs/default. (KB-ZD3W, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- A recursive obsutil copy recreates the source folder name under the destination prefix by default, so the result is nested one level deeper. The -flat option places the contents directly under the destination prefix. (KB-ZE54, live, checked 2026-06-17)

## mrs (107)

- The MRS cluster manager API rejects a PUT that modifies the built-in admin account. (KB-2ZYC, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- The MRS cluster manager API rejects a description field that contains a comma when creating a role or a user. (KB-4AAX, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- The Trino statement API is asynchronous. A caller that only submits the statement records success at submit time, not at query completion, so the outcome needs a separate polling step. (KB-4CS3, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- On an MRS cluster in normal (non-secure) mode the Ranger admin API answers over HTTP on port 6080 under /service/public/v2/api/ and is reachable from inside the cluster only. A cluster in secure mode serves Ranger on a different port. (KB-4G3S, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- Iceberg tables created through MRS Spark register in the MRS Hive Metastore with table_type = ICEBERG and a metadata_location, with previous_metadata_location advancing per commit. That is the catalog the HetuEngine Iceberg connector consumes. (KB-4G6G, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- Every step of a security-hardened MRS cluster build can be driven through the MRS Manager REST API, so the build is scriptable end to end. (KB-4GLX, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- Impala on MRS reads Iceberg tables, prunes partitions and serves DESCRIBE HISTORY and version time travel. An INSERT into a table holding a timestamptz column returns an error, and Spark writes a TIMESTAMP column as timestamptz. (KB-4JTU, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- A five node MRS cluster created through POST /v2/{project_id}/clusters in eu-de took about 23 minutes from the create call to a usable cluster. (KB-4Q3W, live, checked 2026-07-29)
- The request schema of each MRS Manager API endpoint is readable on the Manager node in /opt/Bigdata/om-server/apache-tomcat-*/webapps/web/WEB-INF/validate/validate-rule-*.xml. Those files list, per endpoint and method, every parameter, whether it is required, and its validation regex. (KB-4QC4, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- MapReduce Service (MRS) is a managed big data platform for building and running big data clusters: core nodes process data and store it in HDFS, and task nodes process data and are added or removed by auto scaling rules. The documentation names the engines ClickHouse, Spark, Flink, Kafka and HBase and compatibility with the open-source components. The Regions and Endpoints page lists the MRS API endpoint in eu-de. (KB-4SRI, contract, checked 2026-10-11)
- A Ranger obs policy on MRS that grants to a user returns Obs not support User grants, so grants are made to groups. (KB-4VAD, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- hetu-cli echoes its own java command line, including the --password value in clear text, so the password shows up in the process list and in any captured log. (KB-4VW3, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- GET /web/api/v2/permission/clusters/1/services/{service}/views/{view}/resources needs resource_type and resource_name beside path; path=root alone returns error 12-5000036. With path=root, resource_type=parentQueue and resource_name=root it returns the child queues with their Submit and Admin permissions. (KB-5DZX, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- HetuEngine coordinators read catalogs from the hetumetastore database (catalog.management = HETUENGINE), so a catalog .properties file placed on HSBroker is ignored. The HSConsole DataSourceType enum offers no ICEBERG entry, and registering Iceberg as type HIVE injects hive.allow-* properties the connector rejects. (KB-5FDH, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- Killing the coordinator YARN application leaves the HetuEngine compute instance in state FAULT with 0 nodes, and it does not recover on its own. A rebuild through hetu-cli then gets HTTP 500 from HSBroker because no instance exists. (KB-5KTV, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- An Iceberg data source can still be registered in HetuEngine by repointing an unused type's template in the HSConsole datasource.properties, restarting HetuEngine, then posting to /v1/hsconsole/datasource on port 29880 with a datasourceRequest body. hive.config.resources needs absolute paths, otherwise reads fail with "EndPoint is not set". (KB-5TQ6, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- The MRS create call accepts the default ECS agency field and builds the cluster even when IAM holds no agency under that name yet. (KB-6FHT, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- The HetuEngine data source type field takes an HSConsole enum, which on the date of the check held CARBONDATA, CLICKHOUSE, ELASTICSEARCH, GAUSSDB-A, GBASE, HBASE, HETU_ENGINE, HIVE, HUDI, IOTDB, JDBC, MYSQL and ORACLE. CARBONDATA was rejected on a cluster where that component was not installed. (KB-6MXV, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- The HetuEngine coordinator port changes across service restarts; 29870, 29874 and 29879 were observed on one cluster. The current coordinator URI is published in ZooKeeper under /hetuserver/engine/<tenant-id>/coordinator/<node-uuid> in the field uri. (KB-7EI4, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- MRS node group flavors carry the suffix .linux.mrs, for example m3.2xlarge.8.linux.mrs and c4.4xlarge.4.linux.mrs. (KB-AGYN, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- In the MRS cluster manager v2 API the permission endpoints are /permission/user_roles and /permission/user_groups. The paths /permission/roles and /permission/usergroups both return 404. (KB-AIJX, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- HetuEngine on MRS starts a compute instance only after a Manager role grants Submit and Admin on the YARN queue. The role is created with POST /web/api/v2/permission/user_roles carrying an authModel for the Yarn service and the queue as a childQueue resource. (KB-ANOI, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- Submitting a job to /v2/{project_id}/clusters/{cluster_id}/job-executions returns HTTP 400 with errorCode 12000002 when the submitting IAM user name contains a space. Reading job executions with GET on the same path works with such a user. (KB-AXK3, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- A Ranger obs policy on MRS accepts only the access types read and write. The value list returns error 3022 with the text valid access types=[[read, write]]. (KB-AY4L, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- A shallow Iceberg partition spec on the same 1,152,000 rows produced 96 files of 12,000 rows, 0.3 MB total, 3.1 s to write and 5.5 s to roll up. Point lookups ran at the same speed as on the deep spec, because Iceberg prunes on column statistics as well as on partition paths. (KB-COHI, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- POST /v2/{project_id}/clusters returned 12000002 naming external_datasources when cluster_version was MRS 3.5.0-LTS, and accepted the same field on MRS 3.2.0-LTS.2 in eu-de. (KB-D7FX, live, checked 2026-07-29)
- The body of POST /v1/hsconsole/datasource must be wrapped in a datasourceRequest object; an unwrapped body returns error 400001 reporting that datasourceRequest.datasourcename failed parameter verification. GET /v1/hsconsole/datasource/query lists the registered data sources and DELETE /v1/hsconsole/datasource/{name} removes one. (KB-DMK2, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- POST /v2/{project_id}/clusters in eu-de returned 12000092, Failed to get metadata of version, for cluster_version MRS 3.3.0-LTS.1, a version the API reference listed as current at that time. The same call accepted cluster_version MRS 3.5.0-LTS. (KB-DTTH, live, checked 2026-07-29)
- The HetuEngine HSConsole data source API (POST /v1/hsconsole/datasource) accepts type JDBC for an external relational catalog. The values MYSQL and RDS_MYSQL are rejected with HTTP 400 and the message datasourceRequest.type : Parameter verification failed. (KB-DY4O, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- PUT /permission/users/admin on MRS Manager returns error 12-4000192 about the binding between the password policy and that account. (KB-EBCS, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- On a secured MRS cluster, creating a Ranger policy is refused with 'does not have delegated-admin privilege on given resources', both for the Manager administrator and for a member of the group that the shipped policies grant delegated admin to. (KB-EDGF, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- The HSConsole data source registration is POST /v1/hsconsole/datasource on port 29880, served by a single node of the cluster. A GET on the same path returns HTTP 404. (KB-EJA3, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- On MRS the cluster Manager REST API answers over HTTPS on port 28443 at the Manager floating IP, which is the privateIpFirst field of GET /v1.1/{project_id}/cluster_infos/{cluster_id}. It accepts HTTP basic auth with the Manager administrator account and is reachable from inside the VPC. (KB-FGWH, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- Creating a HetuEngine compute instance needs a YARN queue permission: a Manager role with Submit and Admin on root.default. The CLI message "user does not belong to the specified tenant" is misleading; the HSBroker log states that the user has no permission on default. (KB-FJ2A, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- MRS writes Iceberg data over obs:// into an SSE-KMS encrypted parallel file system without checksum or etag errors. All 14 data objects of the test write carried the KMS key; the unencrypted remainder were zero-byte directory markers, since a parallel file system models real directories. (KB-FN3W, live, checked 2026-07-29)
- The node_size field of POST /v2/{project_id}/clusters takes flavor names ending in .linux.mrs, for example c4.4xlarge.4.linux.mrs. A name ending in .linux.bigdata, the suffix used in documentation examples, returned error 12000048 for an unknown product specification. (KB-FT7O, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- A HetuEngine Iceberg data source takes hive.config.resources as absolute paths, and those files have to exist on every node of the cluster. (KB-GFC4, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- HetuEngine on MRS is a Trino distribution and answers the Trino statement API. A POST to /v1/statement carrying X-Trino-User, X-Trino-Catalog and X-Trino-Schema headers with the SQL as the raw body returns a queued query, and nextUri is polled until the state reaches FINISHED. (KB-GOFP, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- A registered HetuEngine data source is immutable through the API. Posting an existing name returns HTTP 500 with code 600001 and the text The datasource has exist, and DELETE /datasource/{name} answers 404, so a changed configuration is registered under a new name. (KB-GSXI, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- No MySQL JDBC driver ships with HetuEngine, and HSConsole exposes no endpoint for uploading one. The PostgreSQL driver is bundled. A MySQL JDBC data source registers, and the catalog then stays unloaded with MySqlDriverProvider Failed to add driver jar. null in the coordinator log. (KB-HEOG, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- The Ranger service definition for obs on MRS returned resources ['path'] and accessTypes ['read','write'], with rowFilterDef and dataMaskDef both empty, so the definition carries no masking and no row filtering over storage paths. (KB-HO7G, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- GET /v2/{project_id}/clusters/{cluster_id}/job-executions with a limit parameter lists jobs and returns total_record together with job_list. Single job detail includes queue, launcher_id, app_id and tracking_url. (KB-HSOB, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- On a secured MRS cluster Ranger ships hive and trino service definitions, each with eight mask types and row filtering, and the Hive plugin is wired and enforcing. (KB-ICR4, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- A HetuEngine data source created through the API stores the baseProperties field connection-password encrypted, and the coordinator then fails authentication with FATAL: Invalid username/password,login denied. The same credentials placed inside connection-url authenticate, and land in clear text in the catalog properties file in HDFS. (KB-IL2F, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- The Trino Hive connector shipped inside HetuEngine 2.1.0 on MRS carries the partition projection classes and the full property set, with the properties spelled partition_projection_enabled and partition_projection_type. (KB-ITCD, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- On MRS cluster creation a node disk below 480 GB is rejected with MRS.00005022. The check fires on the root_volume field of the create call rather than on the data volume. (KB-IVZR, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- A HetuEngine compute instance (coordinator and workers as YARN service applications) is created headless with hetu-cli --catalog hive --tenant default, after installing the client from the HetuEngine component directory and copying hetuserver-client.properties from the HSBroker etc directory. No web UI step is involved. (KB-IZY2, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- On MRS 3.5.0-LTS the views returned for Yarn, Hive and HDFS are view1 on Yarn (resource root, type parentQueue, auths Submit and Admin), hive_data_operation_priv on Hive (database default, auths select/insert/create/delete/grant_of_*) and view1 on HDFS (hdfs://hacluster/, type Folders, auths read/write/execute). (KB-JB6H, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- The HetuEngine dynamic catalog scanner picks up a newly registered data source in about 60 seconds, with no service restart. (KB-JP3P, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- hetu-cli discovers the coordinator through HSBroker on port 29861. When HSBroker answers 500 the CLI resolves --server to an empty value and every command ends with Missing required parameter for option '--server', which reads like a usage error; the coordinator address can be read from ZooKeeper instead. (KB-JPCL, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- The MRS cluster manager REST API answers on the cluster float IP, on port 28443 and on 9022. Requests to a node's own address, to the loopback interface or to the FQDN return status 000, which reads as the API being switched off. The float IP is in the OMS configuration file as float_ip. (KB-K35K, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- On MRS 3.5.0-LTS Hive takes Athena-style external table DDL unchanged apart from the LOCATION scheme, which is obs:// instead of s3://. Creating a partitioned Parquet external table, adding a partition, running MSCK REPAIR TABLE and listing partitions all succeeded through beeline within seconds. (KB-K5ID, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- The Trino connector in MRS implements partition projection, configured with Trino's own property names. (KB-K6IQ, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- In the HetuEngine client package trino.tar.gz the plugin/* entries are symlinks into trino/shareLib/. Extracting only the plugin path produces dangling links, which makes connector classes look absent; the classes sit under the shareLib path. (KB-KKXD, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- When creating a user through the MRS cluster manager API the field userType must be HM. (KB-KM3X, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- POST /web/api/v2/permission/users creates a Manager user on MRS and returns HTTP 204. Repeating the call for a user that already exists returns HTTP 500 with error 12-5000048. (KB-KPR2, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- MRS rejects job submission when the IAM user name contains spaces, with the message "This operation cannot be performed because your username contains spaces." The message is written only to the job log in the service-owned OBS bucket. (KB-KXYS, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- MRS 3.3.0-LTS.1 and MRS 3.5.0-LTS ship Spark 3.3.1, the Spark version DLI also runs, so a jar built against Spark 3.3.1 needs no Spark version change to run as an MRS spark-submit job. MRS 3.6.0-LTS moves to Spark 3.5.6. (KB-L4MM, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- MRS wrote to an SSE-KMS encrypted parallel file system over the obs:// scheme with the default MRS ECS agency end to end: HDFS put and cat, a partitioned Iceberg table, insert, row-level UPDATE and kept snapshot history, with all 14 data objects carrying the customer KMS key. (KB-L6IL, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- The HetuEngine CLI creates its compute instance itself on the first connection. (KB-LRZR, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- Iceberg on TCP runs on three catalogs: the MRS Hive Metastore, which HetuEngine reads, a JDBC catalog on RDS for PostgreSQL where no MRS cluster exists, and a HadoopCatalog on OBS, which upstream treats as single-writer because S3-compatible storage gives no atomic compare-and-set. (KB-M63N, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- GET /v1/hsconsole/clusters lists the HetuEngine compute instances with id, state, coordinator webui URL and worker counts. POST /v1/hsconsole/clusters/{id}/start (also stop, restart, delete) manages a single instance. (KB-MON7, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- MRS SQL runs asynchronously over POST /v2/{project_id}/clusters/{cluster_id}/job-executions, which returns 202 with job_submit_result.job_id. GET on the same path with the job id returns job_detail carrying job_state, job_result, job_progress and the submitted, started and finished times. (KB-MXUR, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- On an MRS cluster, obs:// access to a parallel file system bucket works through a credentials provider already configured in core-site.xml, so no AK/SK has to be placed on the nodes. (KB-MZ77, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- The sftp subsystem is disabled on MRS cluster nodes, so scp fails with "subsystem request failed on channel 0". Piping over ssh with a redirect into a file on the node works. (KB-NC6T, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- Iceberg rewrite_data_files compacts within a partition, so over-partitioning is not repairable afterwards. A 6-level spec over 1,152,000 rows produced 19,200 partitions holding one 2.7 KB file each, 51.3 MB total, 139.9 s to write, and rewrite_data_files rewrote 0 files. (KB-OFL4, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- HetuEngine 2.1.0 is based on Trino 399, which was released on 6 October 2022. (KB-OI6W, docs, checked 2026-07-30)
- MRS cluster creation creates its own security group for the cluster nodes. An operator host reaches the cluster after an ingress rule for that host is added to this group. (KB-OIOO, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- Impala 4.3.0 on MRS carries iceberg-api 1.2.1 and iceberg-hive-runtime 1.2.1 and handles read, partition pruning, DESCRIBE HISTORY, time travel with FOR SYSTEM_VERSION AS OF, CREATE TABLE STORED BY ICEBERG and INSERT. (KB-OOAR, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- MRS Spark defaults spark.sql.storeAssignmentPolicy to LEGACY, which the DataSource V2 write path refuses; Iceberg writes run with the value set to ANSI. (KB-OUMI, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- An MRS 3.5.0-LTS cluster of 5 nodes takes about 23 minutes from the create call until it is usable. (KB-PTJN, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- core-site.xml on an MRS node already sets fs.obs.security.provider to the MRS credentials provider, and the node metadata service returns live temporary credentials, so an OBS-backed Iceberg warehouse in the Hive Metastore runs with no credentials written into any configuration file. (KB-PWYS, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- MRS Spark on YARN reads Iceberg tables on OBS that were written from a machine outside the cluster, including a schema-evolved column and an evolved partition spec, and writes back with INSERT and MERGE INTO. Snapshot lineage stays intact across engines. (KB-Q4IP, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- The MRS v2 job API has an asynchronous shape: submit a job, poll it by job id, then read the result object. HetuEngine and Impala themselves are reached over synchronous JDBC. (KB-R32Q, docs, checked 2026-07-30)
- The MRS Manager REST API answers on the cluster floating IP address, on port 28443 and identically on port 9022, with basic authentication. Probes against the node hostname, its FQDN or the loopback address returned no HTTP status code. (KB-RDCL, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- On the MRS Manager API, GET /web/api/v2/clusters/1/tenants returned HTTP 405 on the bare collection. The tenant list is read from /web/api/v2/clusters/1/tenants/queue_config/super_scheduler, and /tenants/calc_resource/scheduler_mode returns SUPER or CAPACITY, which is needed to build a tenant creation body. (KB-RMRE, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- On an MRS cluster with HetuEngine the HSConsole REST API listens on port 29880, bound to the business IP of the HSBroker node. It answers plain HTTP and accepted calls without credentials from inside the cluster. (KB-RQV2, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- remove_orphan_files rejects any older_than window shorter than 24 hours, by upstream Iceberg design. A timestamp in the future trips the same check, because the window is measured against the current time. (KB-RUF5, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- For MRS 3.x, including 3.5.0-LTS, the MRS user manual sets a floor of 64 GB for master node memory (most pages say greater than 64 GB, the Doris page at least 64 GB), so 16 GiB and 32 GiB master flavors do not qualify. The MRS 3.5.0-LTS clusters built on eu-de used 64 GiB flavors, m3.2xlarge.8 for master and c4.4xlarge.4 for core nodes. (KB-RY3G, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- impalad on an MRS cluster listens on the core nodes, ports 21050 and 21000, not on the master node. (KB-SGOR, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- On MRS, a HetuEngine query user needs the YARN queue permission and membership in the hetuuser group, and HetuEngine is restarted after that grant. (KB-SHRY, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- spark-sql caches Iceberg table metadata for the life of a session. The files and snapshots metadata tables read right after a maintenance call still return the pre-maintenance numbers; a fresh session showed the compaction result, 5 data files before and 2 after. (KB-SYST, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- MRS cluster nodes run sshd with the SFTP subsystem switched off, so scp to a node returns an error and file transfer goes through a pipe over ssh. (KB-TGSO, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- A secured MRS cluster serves Ranger admin over HTTPS on port 6182 of the RangerAdmin node. An insecure cluster serves it over HTTP on port 6080. (KB-U4XJ, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- With Kerberos enabled, Spark on YARN in MRS creates an Iceberg table on OBS, inserts into it, runs a row-level UPDATE and keeps snapshot history. (KB-UALF, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- Probing an MRS master for the Ranger admin API on ports 21400, 21401 and 6182 got no answer, and port 6085 is bound to the loopback interface only. (KB-UE7F, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- The MRS node metadata service answers "401 Please configure Cloud Service Agency first" when the create-cluster body omitted mrs_ecs_default_agency. The wording resembles a Kerberos restriction, but the cause is the missing field. (KB-UKGD, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- An Iceberg data source registered in HetuEngine after the service restart answers with Catalog not found. The order that works writes the catalog first and restarts the service afterwards. (KB-UM5W, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- GET /web/api/v2/clusters on the MRS Manager returns the cluster id (usually 1), the auth mode and the installed packs. GET /web/api/v2/clusters/1/services lists every service with its role instances and health, and the per-service call reports runningStatus values GOOD, STARTING or STOPPED. (KB-UOVS, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- A HetuEngine user must also belong to the hetuuser group, otherwise the call returns error 10000013 asking for that group. Group membership changes take effect only after a HetuEngine restart. (KB-UWS2, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- A comma inside the description field of an MRS Manager role or user creation request is rejected with 12-4000004 Invalid description. (KB-UZL5, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- A response of 000 from MRS Manager on port 28443 means the probe went to the wrong address. Manager binds to the cluster float IP, not to the address returned by hostname -i on a node. (KB-V4OT, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- HetuEngine picks up a newly registered data source only after the service is restarted, and that restart has to follow the catalog registration in HSConsole. (KB-VHYQ, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- An MRS 3.5.0-LTS node group requires a root_volume of at least 480 GB. (KB-VJES, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- The MRS client environment script bigdata_env references unset variables, so a shell script running with set -u aborts silently when it sources that file. Running with set -o pipefail alone avoids the abort. (KB-VTJC, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- On the MRS v2 job-executions API the job_type values HiveSql, HiveScript, SparkSql and SparkSubmit were seen working in eu-de on 2026-07-30. (KB-VZ4R, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- Killing the default-coordinator and default-worker YARN applications on MRS leaves the HetuEngine compute instance in FAULT with zero nodes, and it stays there. While no instance exists HSBroker answers HTTP 500 on every endpoint, so hetu-cli has no discovery path; recovery goes through the HSConsole Start or Restart action, or a Manager service restart. (KB-W56N, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- On an MRS 3.5.0-LTS cluster with Ranger and without Guardian, GET /service returned exactly one service, of type obs and name obs, holding zero policies. (KB-W5DT, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- Without Guardian installed on an MRS cluster, creating any Ranger obs policy fails with Init obs client failed. (KB-WENQ, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- Each MRS node group accepts only a subset of flavors. On MRS 3.5.0-LTS in eu-de master_node_default_group accepted m3.2xlarge.8.linux.mrs and c4.4xlarge.4.linux.mrs and rejected every 16 GiB and 32 GiB flavor tried; core_node_analysis_group accepted only c4.4xlarge.4.linux.mrs out of six. (KB-X5PJ, live, checked 2026-07-29)
- On MRS 3.5.0-LTS Spark with an Iceberg warehouse on OBS the five Iceberg maintenance procedures rewrite_data_files, rewrite_manifests, expire_snapshots, rewrite_position_delete_files and remove_orphan_files all run successfully. (KB-XBDB, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- The first kinit of a newly created MRS Manager user forces a password change, so old password and new password are both supplied at that first login. (KB-XKVV, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- On a Kerberos-secured MRS cluster the built-in admin identity carries only the ficommon group and the Manager_administrator_180 role, so Spark jobs fail with ACL check failed on root.default and Hive access fails with Permission denied on /user/hive. (KB-YJJP, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- The HetuEngine coordinator runs in a YARN container as user omm, so connector configuration files need read and traverse rights for omm on every node. Where they lack them, the scanner logs Invalid configuration property and reports the file as missing although it is present. (KB-YWYI, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- The MRS Manager REST API together with hetu-cli covers the full HetuEngine compute instance flow, so the steps can be scripted. (KB-ZIOE, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- On a secured MRS cluster the Ranger role instances are listed with GET /web/api/v2/clusters/1/services/Ranger/instances on Manager port 28443, which returns role instances with numeric ids. A RangerAdmin instance is then reached through the Manager proxy on port 9022 under /component/Ranger/RangerAdmin/{instance_id}/{ranger_path}. (KB-ZODF, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- A user created through POST /permission/users on MRS Manager has to carry userType HM. The values HumanMachine and Machine-Machine are rejected with 12-4000013 Invalid user type. (KB-ZVIB, live, checked 2026-07-30)

## iam (102)

- Moving a resource to another enterprise project does not change the resource itself, but it changes who can access it when access is granted per enterprise project: a user who holds permissions only for the source enterprise project can lose access after the move. (KB-27UC, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- On TCP the default IAM quota is 500 users per tenant. GET /v3.0/OS-QUOTA/domains/{domain_id} returned quota 500, min 50 and max 2000 for type user on two test tenants in eu-de on 2026-10-02. (KB-2DEG, live, checked 2026-10-02)
- Role ids for a role assignment are read with GET /v3/roles?per_page=300 and matched on the display_name field. (KB-2PUH, live, checked 2026-09-17)
- Enterprise Project Management Service (EPS) groups cloud resources such as ECS, OBS and VPC into enterprise projects that match departments, business units or environments such as production and test, and gives a unified view of resource management and access control across services and regions. The documentation describes it as a cloud governance platform for managing personnel, resources and permissions by the organizational structure. (KB-2U4K, contract, checked 2026-10-11)
- With an AK/SK-signed request in eu-de, GET /v3/projects on iam.eu-de.otc.t-systems.com lists the projects and sub-projects of the account, while GET /v3/users and GET /v3/groups answer HTTP 403 'You are not authorized to perform the requested action.' A key that can list projects therefore cannot prove whether IAM users or groups exist. (KB-33GL, live, checked 2026-10-04)
- The oidc protocol sub-resource under an IAM identity provider carries the mapping_id of the federation mapping in its request body. (KB-364P, live, checked 2026-08-27)
- On the check date GET /v3/roles with per_page=300 returned about 220 system roles, each with its policy document. GET /v3.0/OS-ROLE/roles returned only the tenant's own custom policies, so system roles and custom policies come from different listings. (KB-3AAE, live, checked 2026-08-19)
- The console forces a password change at the first login of a new user, who shows pwd_status false until then. After that change the password an automation still holds no longer authenticates. (KB-3JTS, live, checked 2026-08-19)
- Identity and Access Management (IAM) is the central service that manages users, user groups and their permissions to T Cloud Public (TCP) resources and services, and controls who (users, groups or federated identities) may access which service and resource under which conditions, such as time, IP address or MFA. The documentation adds federated single sign-on from the customer's identity system, delegation of access to another account or a cloud service, and security policies for multi-factor authentication, login authentication, passwords and access control lists. The Regions and Endpoints page lists API endpoints for IAM in eu-de and eu-nl. (KB-424Q, contract, checked 2026-10-11)
- The IAM federation API uses two spellings: v3 paths use identity_providers with an underscore, v3.0 paths use identity-providers with a hyphen. (KB-46TQ, live, checked 2026-08-27)
- The MRS create-cluster API accepts MRS_ECS_DEFAULT_AGENCY even before that IAM agency exists and provisions it. Creating an agency directly needs identity:createAgencyEx, which Security Administrator holds and a normal AK/SK user lacks. (KB-47IL, live, checked 2026-07-28)
- The OIDC configuration of one identity provider is read with GET /v3.0/OS-FEDERATION/identity-providers/{idp_id}/openid-connect-config. It answers HTTP 200 with access_mode, either program or program_console, idp_url as the issuer, client_id as the audience and signing_key. (KB-47JM, live, checked 2026-07-02)
- Six fine-grained system roles on the eu-de project are enough to build a VPC + NAT + ELB v3 + ECS stack with OpenTofu: ECS FullAccess, VPC FullAccess (which covers EIPs and security groups), ELB FullAccess, NAT FullAccess, EVS FullAccess and IMS ReadOnlyAccess. (KB-4EVM, live, checked 2026-09-17)
- POST /v1.0/enterprise-projects/{id}/resources/filter with resource_types and projects in the body returns 200 and lists the resources held by that enterprise project. Region-level resources are returned only when the projects field is set. (KB-4OYO, live, checked 2026-06-02)
- An AK/SK-signed GET https://iam.eu-de.otc.t-systems.com/v3/projects returns HTTP 200 with the project list when the key is valid, which tests a key in one call without creating anything. (KB-4SSK, live, checked 2026-08-18)
- An enterprise project of TCP cannot be deleted by the tenant, neither in the console nor through the API: it can only be disabled. A disabled enterprise project still counts against the enterprise project quota and cannot be modified. Only the operator's O&M can enable deletion for a tenant, on a support request. (KB-57EM, docs, checked 2026-10-06)
- The signing key of an OIDC identity provider is held as a stored static JWKS in the provider configuration. The platform does not fetch the issuer jwks_uri at exchange time, so a key rotation at the external provider breaks the token exchange until the stored key is refreshed. (KB-5CFW, live, checked 2026-07-02)
- A token from POST /v3/auth/tokens is returned in the X-Subject-Token response header and has a validity period of 24 hours, but it becomes invalid within 30 minutes after the IAM user is deleted or disabled or its password, access key or permissions change. (KB-5FOB, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- POST /v3.0/OS-CREDENTIAL/credentials with a user_id in the body returns a credential object holding the access key and the secret key. The secret appears in that response only. (KB-6H6V, live, checked 2026-06-17)
- The current OIDC signing key of an identity provider is returned by GET /v3.0/OS-FEDERATION/identity-providers/{idp_id}/openid-connect-config. The same path under /v3 answers HTTP 404 APIGW.0101 with no configuration, so a caller that ignores the status sees no key and rotates on every run. (KB-6LSL, live, checked 2026-09-25)
- GET /v3/OS-FEDERATION/identity_providers on the IAM endpoint answers HTTP 200 and lists the identity providers configured for the account, each with its description text. (KB-6MBW, live, checked 2026-07-02)
- A new IAM user added to existing groups that already carry object-storage rights reached a bucket at once: HEAD bucket answered 200 on the first try, seconds after the group membership was set. (KB-736M, live, checked 2026-09-23)
- A FunctionGraph function configured with an agency that is delegated to a different service is refused with FSS.1501 "The delegate not exist". The agency has to be delegated to FunctionGraph itself. (KB-774P, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- OpenStack projects (OP) group and isolate the compute, storage and network resources of one tenant into separate projects, and each project can be assigned to users and to user groups. (KB-7IGI, contract, checked 2026-10-11)
- The Config resource recorder needs an IAM agency that trusts the service domain op_svc_rms, created with POST /v3.0/OS-AGENCY/agencies and the field trust_domain_name. The OBS writability check of the recorder appears to run under that service identity rather than under the caller. (KB-7XJY, live, checked 2026-07-17)
- On T Cloud Public (TCP) in eu-de the Enterprise Project Service is domain-level: a request to /v1.0/enterprise-projects signed with AK/SK, or carrying a project-scoped IAM token, returns HTTP 401 with error code EPS.0003. A domain-scoped IAM token is accepted. (KB-A2NP, live, checked 2026-06-02)
- After an access key is deleted in IAM, a signed API call made with that key returns HTTP 401. (KB-AJJH, live, checked 2026-08-24)
- The IAM policy actions for the Enterprise Project Service are eps:enterpriseProjects:list, create, get, update, enable and disable, plus eps:resources:list, add and remove. (KB-AOXY, docs, checked 2026-06-02)
- An IAM agency delegates permissions to another account on the same platform or to a platform service. Identity from another cloud or from an enterprise directory is integrated through an IAM identity provider with SAML 2.0 or OIDC. (KB-AVKS, docs, checked 2026-09-01)
- A FunctionGraph function gets credentials from an IAM agency delegated to the service account op_svc_cff and bound to the function. Inside the function, context.getToken() returns an agency-scoped IAM token for the X-Auth-Token header, so no access key or password is stored. (KB-BU5C, live, checked 2026-07-17)
- With access_mode set to programmatic the console login form refuses the user while API access with a token and signed AK/SK calls keep working. GET /v3.0/OS-USER/users/{user_id} returns access_mode, is_domain_owner, xuser_type and last_pwd_auth_time. (KB-C273, live, checked 2026-08-19)
- GET /v1.0/enterprise-projects/providers?limit=200 returned 200 with 46 supported services and total_count 46 on 2026-09-25, eu-de. Earlier checks showed 44 on 2026-06-02 and 43 on 2026-07-23, so the returned set changes between checks. (KB-COAJ, live, checked 2026-09-25)
- A read-only AK/SK in a tenant used by several teams returned the VPCs, subnets, servers and floating addresses created by all of them. An inventory built on the raw listings held 35 of 56 objects belonging to other teams. (KB-D4J4, live, checked 2026-09-17)
- T Cloud Public (TCP) manages users, user groups and federation in Identity and Access Management (IAM): administrators create users for people, systems and applications, put them in groups that carry the permissions, and connect the enterprise identity provider for single sign-on via SAML (virtual users or IAM users) or OpenID Connect. The OIDC configuration of an identity provider was read live through the IAM federation API (KB-47JM). (KB-D64T, docs, checked 2026-10-11)
- The EPS v1.0 API in eu-de offers list, create, query, update, enable and disable for an enterprise project. It exposes no delete operation, so an enterprise project can only be disabled. (KB-DACZ, docs, checked 2026-06-02)
- GET /v3.0/OS-CREDENTIAL/credentials/{ak} returns the owning user_id, the status and the create_time of an access key. (KB-DH33, live, checked 2026-08-18)
- In the password authentication body of POST /v3/auth/tokens the user's domain goes under the key domain. Using the key domain_name instead produces HTTP 401, which reads like a wrong user name or password. (KB-DHCO, live, checked 2026-07-29)
- Enterprise Project Service is a domain-level service in eu-de reached at https://eps.eu-de.otc.t-systems.com/v1.0/enterprise-projects. Its API accepts a domain-scoped IAM token only, sent as X-Auth-Token; AK/SK signing and project-scoped tokens are rejected with 401 EPS.0003. (KB-DHDH, live, checked 2026-06-03)
- GET /v3/projects?name=eu-de on iam.eu-de.otc.t-systems.com returns 200 with a projects array holding the region project name and its id. The call is AK/SK-signed like any other, so a project id can be resolved without a token and without the password flow. (KB-EAG6, live, checked 2026-08-18)
- An access key can return HTTP 200 on read calls across IMS, VPC, ECS, EVS, EIP, KMS and OBS and still fail on a create call. (KB-EP4A, live, checked 2026-08-18)
- TMS and EPS calls made with project-scoped credentials return HTTP 401 in TCP. Both APIs accept domain-scoped credentials. (KB-EPXJ, live, checked 2026-09-25)
- GET /v3/projects on iam.eu-de.otc.t-systems.com is the cheapest credential test. A live AK/SK returns 200 with the project list, a deleted or wrong key returns 401 APIGW.0301 "Incorrect IAM authentication information". (KB-FHGI, live, checked 2026-08-18)
- IAM lists three system roles named OBS Administrator in eu-de, two of type XX and one of type AX. The type AX role accepted a domain-level group assignment: PUT /v3/domains/{domain_id}/groups/{group_id}/roles/{role_id} returned 204. (KB-FLAD, live, checked 2026-09-23)
- IAM allows at most two access keys per user in eu-de. When both slots are in use, one existing key has to be deleted before a new one can be created. (KB-H5QL, live, checked 2026-08-24)
- An IAM user on TCP holds at most two permanent access keys, and creating a third fails with HTTP 400 akSkNumExceed. At that cap one key is deleted before the new one is created while the other key stays valid, and the API reference lists a user token (X-Auth-Token) as mandatory for both key calls. (KB-HBZF, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- PUT /v3.0/OS-MFA/mfa-devices/bind and PUT /v3.0/OS-MFA/mfa-devices/unbind take a flat JSON body; a nested body returns 400 with the message Request body is invalid. A bind supplies two consecutive TOTP codes and answers 204. (KB-HMTS, live, checked 2026-08-19)
- IAM user and credential calls need a password-based, domain-scoped token from POST iam/v3/auth/tokens, returned in the X-Subject-Token response header. POST /v3.0/OS-CREDENTIAL/credentials then returns an access key and its secret, and the secret appears only in that response. (KB-HRK7, live, checked 2026-06-17)
- GET /v3.0/OS-USER/users/{user_id}/login-protect returns 404 with error code IAM.0004 for a freshly created user whose login protection was never configured. (KB-HRNW, live, checked 2026-08-19)
- DELETE /v3.0/OS-CREDENTIAL/credentials/{access_key} answers 204 and revokes the key at once. The next signed call with that key returned 401 with error code APIGW.0301 saying the ak does not exist, so there is no grace period. (KB-JEWE, live, checked 2026-08-24)
- A service validates a request before it authorises it. With a read-only key, a DELETE of a nonexistent id answered 404 and a POST with an empty body answered 400, so neither answer tells anything about the key's permissions. (KB-JNYO, live, checked 2026-09-17)
- The service calls of a region-scoped deployment need the region's project id, which IAM returns for an AK/SK-signed GET /v3/projects?name=eu-de. (KB-JQOG, live, checked 2026-08-18)
- GET /v3/auth/domains on the IAM endpoint returns the account domain id. That id is required as the domain_id query parameter of GET /v3.0/OS-AGENCY/agencies, which lists the agencies of the account. (KB-JXAY, live, checked 2026-07-02)
- The access key calls under /v3.0/OS-CREDENTIAL run with a domain-scoped token obtained from POST /v3/auth/tokens. (KB-JZJP, live, checked 2026-08-24)
- GET /v3.0/OS-CREDENTIAL/credentials/{access_key} returns the user_id that owns that access key, which maps a key to its IAM user without a lookup by user name. (KB-LC2D, live, checked 2026-08-24)
- On TCP the IAM quota response lists enterprise project grants as types assigment_group_ep, assigment_user_ep and assigment_agency_ep with quota 500 each, and grants on multiple projects as assigment_group_mp and assigment_agency_mp with quota 200 each. Seen on two test tenants in eu-de on 2026-10-02. (KB-LC5E, live, checked 2026-10-02)
- A read-only console key needs CCE ReadOnlyAccess to list clusters and nodes plus the ReadOnlyAccess role of every other service it reads. With only those roles a VPC create sent with a valid body was refused. (KB-LQ2Y, live, checked 2026-09-17)
- Effective permissions are cumulative. IAM-project policies are evaluated before enterprise-project policies and the first Allow wins, so a narrow enterprise-project grant cannot cap a broader IAM grant held by the same user or group. (KB-LTXL, docs, checked 2026-07-15)
- The default login policy locks an account after five failed attempts and keeps it locked for 15 minutes; requests then return 401 with the message The account is locked. (KB-M5XE, live, checked 2026-08-19)
- POST /v3.0/OS-MFA/virtual-mfa-devices has to carry the token of the user the device is created for; the same call with an administrator token returns 403. The successful response holds base32_string_seed and serial_number. (KB-MSQX, live, checked 2026-08-19)
- On TCP the default IAM quota is 128 custom policies per tenant. GET /v3.0/OS-QUOTA/domains/{domain_id} returned quota 128, min 128 and max 5000 for type policy on two test tenants in eu-de on 2026-10-02. (KB-N4IJ, live, checked 2026-10-02)
- IAM management calls signed with an AK/SK pair, such as POST /v3/groups, return 403 with the message 'You are not authorized to perform the requested action' even for a member of the admin group, unless the request carries the X-Domain-Id header with the account id. With that header the same call returns 201. Reads of the caller's own credential and user work without it. (KB-N66Y, live, checked 2026-09-23)
- POST /v3/auth/tokens accepts the identity methods password and totp together and returns 201 with mfa_authn_at in the body. Only verification_method vmfa gates this token API: with sms or email a password-only token request still returns 201, while vmfa answers 401 mfa totp code verify fail. (KB-NCCP, live, checked 2026-08-19)
- IAM user passwords expire after about 90 days by default, which breaks integrations that authenticate with a user password. On OpenShift this shows up as HTTP 401 against EVS and stops Cinder CSI volume provisioning until the password and the stored credentials secret are rotated. (KB-NPZE, docs, checked 2026-06-08)
- An IAM user gets enterprise project access from the Enterprise Management administrator policy, which is a global-service policy. The action names are eps:enterpriseProjects:* and eps:resources:*. (KB-OD7A, docs, checked 2026-06-03)
- Per-user login protection is set with PUT /v3.0/OS-USER/users/{user_id}/login-protect and takes verification_method sms, email or vmfa. GET /v3.0/OS-USER/login-protects lists only users that were ever configured, and the value none was seen in that listing. (KB-ONOY, live, checked 2026-08-19)
- A request signed with an access key that was deleted in IAM returns HTTP 401 with error_code APIGW.0301 and error_msg "Incorrect IAM authentication information: ak <key> not exist". The wording identifies a missing key, not a wrong signature. (KB-OQZL, live, checked 2026-08-18)
- In the IAM role catalog the display_name carries the meaning: the role shown as OBS Administrator appears under system_all_34 and system_all_35, while a similar internal name such as system_all_64 belongs to an unrelated service. Matching is done on display_name. (KB-OWB7, live, checked 2026-07-17)
- In the EPS providers listing total_count is not reliable with a small limit: at limit=5 the response reported total_count 4, while limit=200 returned the full set with a matching count. (KB-P7Z6, live, checked 2026-06-02)
- POST /v3.0/OS-CREDENTIAL/securitytokens with identity method token returns 201, but the response carries no scope information and a project-scoped endpoint called with that credential answers 400 Common.0018, tenantId in token is not the same with in URL, whatever scope the source token had. (KB-PADJ, live, checked 2026-08-19)
- POST /v1.0/enterprise-projects/{id}/resources-migrate with resource_type, resource_id and project_id returns 204 and moves an existing resource into that enterprise project. Passing id 0 moves the resource back to the default enterprise project. The round trip was run with a VPC. (KB-PBX6, live, checked 2026-06-03)
- A user with no IAM policy attached can still obtain a project-scoped token, but an EIP create call from that user returns HTTP 403 PolicyNotAuthorized. (KB-PKZG, live, checked 2026-07-17)
- A signed IAM API call that returns HTTP 401 with error code APIGW.0301 and an ak not exist message points to an access key deleted in IAM, not to a signing error. (KB-PPUV, live, checked 2026-08-18)
- DELETE /v3/users/{user_id} on the IAM endpoint answers HTTP 204 and removes the user together with the access keys issued to that user. (KB-QD7I, live, checked 2026-06-17)
- A user created with POST /v3/users needs a few seconds before it can authenticate. A token request issued immediately returns 401 with the message The username or password is wrong, while a wait of about five seconds was enough. (KB-QEHQ, live, checked 2026-08-19)
- PUT /v3.0/OS-USER/users/{user_id} with {"user":{"access_mode":"programmatic"}} answers 200 right after POST /v3/users, and an access key created with POST /v3.0/OS-CREDENTIAL/credentials was usable about 10 seconds after creation. (KB-QO6I, live, checked 2026-09-17)
- PUT /v3.0/OS-USER/users/{user_id} sets access_mode to console, programmatic or default. With console a password token request returns 403 saying the user only supports console access, and an access key is still created with 201 although it does not work. (KB-QP2C, live, checked 2026-08-19)
- On TCP the v3.0 identity-providers path serves only the openid-connect-config sub-resource. List and get of identity-providers under /v3.0/OS-FEDERATION return 404 APIGW.0101, so providers are enumerated through the v3 path and OIDC is configured through v3.0. (KB-QWLC, live, checked 2026-08-27)
- OIDC identity federation on TCP needs four calls in order: PUT /v3/OS-FEDERATION/identity_providers/{name}, PUT /v3/OS-FEDERATION/mappings/{id}, PUT /v3/OS-FEDERATION/identity_providers/{name}/protocols/oidc, then POST /v3.0/OS-FEDERATION/identity-providers/{name}/openid-connect-config. Skipping the protocol call makes the final call return 404 IAM.0004, "Could not find federated protocol oidc". (KB-REQO, live, checked 2026-08-27)
- Signing a TCP API request needs both halves of the pair: the access key ID (AK) identifies the IAM user and travels in the signed request, while the secret access key (SK) is the secret that produces the signature. (KB-RGQ3, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- POST /v3/auth/tokens on the IAM endpoint returns the token in the X-Subject-Token response header. Later API calls send that value in the X-Auth-Token request header. (KB-S4NP, live, checked 2026-07-29)
- T Cloud Public (TCP) supports multi-factor authentication in Identity and Access Management (IAM): users bind a virtual MFA device, an app that generates 6-digit TOTP codes, and login protection asks for an MFA, SMS or email code after the password. Token requests that combine password and TOTP were tested live (KB-NCCP). (KB-TFIN, docs, checked 2026-10-11)
- Enabling the Config/RMS resource recorder takes two steps in order: an IAM agency trusted to the service account op_svc_rms is created first, then the tracker-config call turns the recorder on. (KB-TIBM, live, checked 2026-07-17)
- A domain-scoped password token carries secu_admin in its role list and created an agency through POST /v3.0/OS-AGENCY/agencies on the first attempt. For the same identity an AK/SK signed request returned 403 identity:createAgencyEx and a project-scoped password token returned 403. (KB-TR6U, live, checked 2026-07-29)
- For the same call, AK/SK, a project-scoped token and a domain-scoped token can return different results. The secu_admin role is carried only by a domain-scoped token. (KB-UDOY, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- On TCP the default IAM quota is 10 identity providers and 10 mappings per tenant. GET /v3.0/OS-QUOTA/domains/{domain_id} returned quota 10 for type idp (max 1000) and type mapping (max 100) on two test tenants in eu-de on 2026-10-02. (KB-UNDV, live, checked 2026-10-02)
- Unbinding a virtual MFA device while login protection with verification_method vmfa is still enabled returns 400 with error code IAM.1062. (KB-V6W3, live, checked 2026-08-19)
- GET /v3.0/OS-CREDENTIAL/credentials/{access_key} returns user_id, status and create_time for one key, and GET /v3.0/OS-CREDENTIAL/credentials?user_id={user_id} lists every key of that user with its description. Both are called with a domain-scoped token in the X-Auth-Token header. (KB-VCXE, live, checked 2026-08-24)
- The authorization engine evaluates IAM-project policies before enterprise-project policies and returns on the first Allow. Effective access is cumulative, so a group holding a tenant-wide OBS grant reaches every bucket regardless of an enterprise-project scoped grant. (KB-VT5H, docs, checked 2026-07-15)
- The id_token to IAM token exchange lives at /v3.0/OS-AUTH/id-token/tokens on eu-de and accepts POST only. A GET on the same path answered HTTP 404 with error code APIGW.0101. The request sends auth.id_token.id, an optional auth.scope.project and the header X-Idp-Id. (KB-W3ZA, live, checked 2026-07-02)
- POST /v3.0/OS-CREDENTIAL/credentials answers 201 with credential.access and credential.secret, and the secret appears in that response only. A description given at creation is stored and shown again in the credential listing. (KB-WWOG, live, checked 2026-08-24)
- A scoped temporary credential comes from POST /v3.0/OS-CREDENTIAL/securitytokens with method assume_role over an agency created by POST /v3.0/OS-AGENCY/agencies, where a scope block is accepted. Without iam:tokens:assume inside the scope of the calling token the request returns 403. In the resulting credential g:MFAPresent stays false. (KB-WY6E, live, checked 2026-08-19)
- The TMS, EPS and RMS (Config) APIs in eu-de accept only a domain-scoped token. The same requests signed with AK/SK and the same requests carrying a project-scoped token were rejected. (KB-X5D6, live, checked 2026-07-17)
- GET /v3.0/OS-CREDENTIAL/credentials/{access_key} also works with an SDK-HMAC-SHA256 signature made with that same key and no token, and returns the user_id that owns the key. (KB-XPIR, live, checked 2026-09-23)
- On 2026-10-02 the read key and the lab key of a TCP test tenant got HTTP 403 IAM.0002 on GET of the IAM password policy and the login policy. The test keys cannot read the tenant security policy. (KB-XQJ7, live, checked 2026-10-02)
- On TCP an IAM sub-project carries the region project as parent_id and a name that starts with the region and an underscore. GET /v3/auth/projects on a test tenant in eu-de on 2026-10-02 listed one such sub-project under the eu-de project. (KB-YDZR, live, checked 2026-10-02)
- Custom policies are created with POST /v3.0/OS-ROLE/roles, type XA for project level and AX for domain level. Attaching works with PUT /v3/projects/{project_id}/groups/{group_id}/roles/{role_id} or the /v3/domains equivalent, both 204. The path /v3.0/OS-ROLE/domains/{domain_id}/groups/{group_id}/roles/{role_id} returns 404 and leaves no assignment behind. (KB-YEPT, live, checked 2026-08-19)
- On TCP the default IAM quota is 50 agencies per tenant. GET /v3.0/OS-QUOTA/domains/{domain_id} returned quota 50, min 10 and max 500 for type agency on two test tenants in eu-de on 2026-10-02. (KB-YFYL, live, checked 2026-10-02)
- An IAM user can hold at most two access keys. At that limit one existing key has to be deleted before POST /v3.0/OS-CREDENTIAL/credentials creates a new one, so a rotation removes the old key first. (KB-ZELI, live, checked 2026-08-24)
- On T Cloud Public (TCP) in eu-de the default enterprise project has id 0 and name default. (KB-ZHAE, live, checked 2026-06-02)
- On TCP the default IAM quota is 20 user groups per tenant. GET /v3.0/OS-QUOTA/domains/{domain_id} returned quota 20, min 10 and max 2000 for type group on two test tenants in eu-de on 2026-10-02. (KB-ZR3C, live, checked 2026-10-02)
- Creating an IAM user with POST /v3/users and its permanent key with POST /v3.0/OS-CREDENTIAL/credentials is done with a domain scoped password token sent in the X-Auth-Token header. That token comes from POST /v3/auth/tokens, and the token response carries the domain id at token.domain.id. (KB-ZS5R, live, checked 2026-06-17)
- GET /v1.0/enterprise-projects/quotas returns 200 and reported a quota of 100 enterprise projects per account in eu-de. (KB-ZTRM, live, checked 2026-06-02)

## ims (82)

- A private image is removed with DELETE /v2/images/{image_id} on the Glance-compatible IMS surface in eu-de, which answers HTTP 204. (KB-23TF, live, checked 2026-06-12)
- Private images of a tenant are listed with GET /v2/cloudimages?__imagetype=private on the IMS endpoint in eu-de, optionally narrowed with a name parameter. The image type parameter carries a double underscore prefix. (KB-2L2B, live, checked 2026-09-25)
- Up to three data-disk images can be registered in one IMS import. (KB-3WRH, docs, checked 2026-06-12)
- A recorded run created a system-disk image from an ACTIVE ECS with a root disk of about 60 GB in roughly 6.5 minutes in eu-de. (KB-475G, live, checked 2026-05-22)
- Adding a support tag to an image needs the glance-native PATCH /v2/images/{id} with Content-Type application/openstack-images-v2.1-json-patch. PATCH /v2/cloudimages/{id} with application/json returns IMG.0001. (KB-4OZA, live, checked 2026-07-21)
- An uploaded image file is converted to the internal zvhd2 format regardless of the source format, confirmed for a qcow2 upload. The registered image's disk_format field reports that internal format, not the type of the file that was uploaded. (KB-5HII, live, checked 2026-07-23)
- An ECS created from an encrypted image is encrypted with the key of the image, and an image created from an encrypted ECS keeps the key of the ECS, while an image registered from an external file is encrypted only when KMS encryption is selected at registration. (KB-5MQE, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- An ECS is captured as a private image with POST /v2/cloudimages/action carrying name and instance_id, which returns a job_id. The value entities.image_id appears in GET /v1/{project_id}/jobs/{job_id} while the job still reads RUNNING, and a 6 to 40 GB system disk reached active in about 90 seconds. (KB-5UCW, live, checked 2026-07-20)
- IMS creates a system-disk image from an existing ECS with POST /v2/cloudimages/action on ims.eu-de.otc.t-systems.com, body with name, description and instance_id. The source server may be running or stopped. The response carries job_id, polled at the IMS jobs endpoint. (KB-6ELY, live, checked 2026-05-22)
- A Glance property PATCH that adds __support_kvm together with the hi1822 properties hiovs, hisriov and hivirtionet applies cleanly even when a property is already present. No HTTP 409 was returned. (KB-6ITH, live, checked 2026-07-21)
- IMS direct import accepts up to 128 GB per disk. A file between 128 GB and 1 TB has to be RAW with a CreateMF.jar bitmap or ZVHD2 with quick import, and a pre-allocated monolithicFlat VMDK has to be converted before import. (KB-6Y34, docs, checked 2026-06-12)
- Private images of a tenant are listed with GET /v2/cloudimages?__imagetype=private. Each returned image reports its size in bytes in the field __image_size. (KB-7ICA, live, checked 2026-07-09)
- An image is shared across tenants by the owner with POST /v1/cloudimages/members on ims.eu-de.otc.t-systems.com, naming the image and the recipient project. The recipient accepts with PUT on the same path with status accepted. Both calls returned 200 with a job_id. (KB-7JZT, live, checked 2026-07-21)
- An IMS system disk image is created from a running instance with POST /v2/cloudimages/action carrying instance_id, and the image reaches state active before the source instance may be deleted. (KB-7X4U, live, checked 2026-07-29)
- An external image file is registered by uploading it to OBS, calling POST /v2/cloudimages/action with os_version set, then polling GET /v1/{project_id}/jobs/{job_id}. A 20 MiB VMDK reached active in about four minutes, and an ECS booted from the result in about 60 seconds. (KB-A4ZN, live, checked 2026-06-12)
- A 20 MiB streamOptimized VMDK imported through IMS in eu-de needed 4 minutes 7 seconds from submission to job state SUCCESS. (KB-AMZY, live, checked 2026-06-12)
- An ECS created from an ISO image registered in IMS is a temporary ECS, and the IMS user guide says disks cannot be attached to it. A backup tool's recovery ISO booted this way cannot be pointed at attached empty data disks to restore into. (KB-B475, docs, checked 2026-10-09)
- In the IMS list of supported OSs for imported private images on TCP, the newest client Windows is Windows 10 64bit. Uploads of unlisted OSs may fail. (KB-BCCA, docs, checked 2026-06-10)
- VirtIO drivers are installed inside the guest while it still runs on the source hypervisor, over the hypervisor console: the install swaps the NIC driver and drops a remote desktop session. The documented package source is the Fedora archive. (KB-BGSF, docs, checked 2026-09-21)
- An IMS register call without os_version lands the image as Other(64 bit) with __platform Other, which loses targeted driver and configuration handling for the guest. (KB-BLW3, live, checked 2026-06-12)
- The external image chain works end to end on TCP: a streamOptimized VMDK produced with qemu-img is uploaded to OBS, registered with IMS and boots as an ECS reachable over SSH. (KB-C3NV, live, checked 2026-06-12)
- An IMS system-disk image created with POST /v2/cloudimages/action and an instance_id, and a CBR backup, both sit outside the EVS disk lifecycle and survive an ECS teardown. (KB-C4YY, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- An IMS import has a floor of about four minutes regardless of file size. A 20 MiB file took 4 minutes 7 seconds. (KB-CHLR, live, checked 2026-06-12)
- The checksum field of an image imported into IMS from an OBS file held the MD5 of the two characters {}, not a hash taken over the file. (KB-CIHR, live, checked 2026-10-01)
- An IMS import with is_config set to true runs automatic configuration, which for Linux images deletes VMware Tools services and copies built-in VirtIO drivers into initrd or initramfs, with documented exceptions for the driver copy such as a separate /usr partition or CentOS 8.0 on XFS. (KB-CKIK, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- IMS fast import (Enable Fast Create, RAW or ZVHD2 up to 1 TB) performs no driver injection, unlike the normal import up to 128 GB. A Windows disk above 128 GB therefore reaches TCP only with the VirtIO drivers it already carries from the source. (KB-CM6Z, docs, checked 2026-10-09)
- A dynamic VHDX file is accepted as an external image file. A 72 MiB VHDX registered to active in 104 seconds, and an ECS created from it reached ACTIVE with a DHCP address in 30 seconds. (KB-CUSR, live, checked 2026-09-02)
- IMS cannot export a private image created from a Windows, SUSE, Red Hat, Ubuntu or Oracle Linux public image, and it cannot export full-ECS images or ISO images either. (KB-CVA4, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- POST /v2/cloudimages/action on the IMS endpoint with name and instance_id answers HTTP 200 with a job_id and queues an image of the server system disk. The image covers that system disk, and data disks of the same server stay outside it. (KB-EACF, live, checked 2026-06-23)
- A Full-ECS image, which bundles the system disk together with attached data disks, uses POST /v1/cloudimages/wholeimages/action with the same body shape as the system disk image call POST /v2/cloudimages/action plus vault_id naming a CBR vault. (KB-F54F, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- An IMS import that read its source file from a Standard-class OBS bucket in the same region succeeded in eu-de with a min_disk of 10 GB. The console import form requires at least 40 GB for the same operation. (KB-FFWI, live, checked 2026-06-12)
- The platform converts an external image on ingest. An image registered from a VHDX file reports disk_format zvhd2, not vhdx, so the uploaded format is not the stored format. (KB-FMM7, live, checked 2026-09-02)
- PATCH /v2/cloudimages/{image_id} on IMS answers HTTP 400 IMG.0001 to a remove operation without a value field. The same remove with "value": "" deletes the attribute. (KB-FZJP, live, checked 2026-10-01)
- An external image file must be uploaded to an OBS bucket in the same region as the image that is registered from it. (KB-GJIJ, docs, checked 2026-07-30)
- Public image names in TCP eu-de follow Standard_Ubuntu_24.04_* for Ubuntu 24, Enterprise_RedHat_9_* for RHEL 9 and Enterprise_Windows-Server_* for Windows Server, as listed on 2026-09-25. (KB-GP36, live, checked 2026-09-25)
- An ECS rebuilt from an IMS image keeps the installed software but presents a new machine identity to a node-locked license manager, which requires a fresh activation. A license left on a deleted instance needs a vendor reset to recover. (KB-GTQH, live, checked 2026-06-17)
- Section 3.10.1 of the TCP service description (revision 17.08.2026) provides public images and GPU drivers for use on T Cloud Public only and prohibits downloading, copying, storing or using them in other environments. (KB-HQFZ, contract, checked 2026-09-25)
- On 2026-10-05 the eu-de public image Standard_Ubuntu_24.04_amd64_bios_latest (also as _uefi_latest) carried __support_kvm and __support_kvm_hi1822_hiovs true, so it fits both s3 flavors (condition __support_kvm) and s9 flavors (condition __support_kvm plus hi1822_hiovs); min_disk 4 GB, min_ram 1 GB. (KB-HTPH, live, checked 2026-10-05)
- IMS documentation requires VMware Tools to be fully uninstalled from a Windows source image for the guest driver installation to succeed after import. (KB-HV27, docs, checked 2026-06-12)
- IMS automatic configuration of a Windows image file restores the IDE driver and DHCP and regenerates the mouse and keyboard registry keys, and it tries an offline VirtIO driver injection. That injection does not work for UEFI images (such as Hyper-V Generation 2 VMs) and can fail with Group Policy or antivirus software, so it is no substitute for drivers installed on the source. (KB-IDSJ, docs, checked 2026-10-09)
- The documentation lists Windows Server 2012, 2012 R2, 2016, 2019 and 2022 among the guest operating systems that support UEFI boot mode. (KB-IHCW, docs, checked 2026-09-21)
- Image support tags are changed with PATCH /v2/images/{image_id} on ims.eu-de.otc.t-systems.com using Content-Type application/openstack-images-v2.1-json-patch, where op add, remove and replace all work. The same PATCH against /v2/cloudimages/{image_id} with application/json returns IMG.0001 about an invalid request message format. (KB-JFK4, live, checked 2026-07-20)
- The IMS image attribute virtual_env_type takes only the values FusionCompute, Ironic, DataImage and IsoImage. A normal virtual ECS system-disk image carries FusionCompute, so that value is not a symptom of a faulty import. (KB-JKZY, docs, checked 2026-07-30)
- An IMS import from an OBS file takes hw_firmware_type uefi in the body of POST /v2/cloudimages/action. The image becomes active with hw_firmware_type uefi. (KB-JLKQ, live, checked 2026-10-01)
- A Windows, SUSE, Red Hat, Ubuntu or Oracle Linux private image that was created from an external image file can be exported, unlike one built from the matching public image. (KB-JN7G, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- TCP has no FreeBSD public image and no FreeBSD price tier in eu-de. (KB-KVBP, live, checked 2026-09-25)
- Image Management Service (IMS) manages the operating system images from which Elastic Cloud Servers are created: public images provided by Telekom, private images the customer creates from a server or uploads as an image file, and images shared between tenants. The public images named in the service description are SUSE Linux Enterprise Server, Oracle Linux, Red Hat Enterprise Linux and Microsoft Windows Server. The Regions and Endpoints page lists API endpoints for IMS in eu-de and eu-nl. (KB-LC4V, contract, checked 2026-10-11)
- Windows Server 2025 public images are live in eu-de. A listing of Windows public images in that region returned 22 images, all Standard edition, marked STD or STD-CORE. (KB-M3JR, live, checked 2026-09-25)
- A private image created from a running ECS inherits the source image's __support_kvm tags and its hw_firmware_type. (KB-MPY3, live, checked 2026-07-20)
- The image import path is decided per single disk file, not per VM: up to 128 GB the supported formats import directly, from 128 GB to 1 TB only RAW with a bitmap file or ZVHD2, and 1 TB is the documented maximum for one disk file. (KB-MXW6, docs, checked 2026-09-21)
- After acceptance the recipient finds the image with GET /v2/cloudimages?id=<image id>, where it reports __imagetype=shared and keeps the same image id as in the owner tenant. An ECS can be created from it. (KB-N7EM, live, checked 2026-07-21)
- On an IMS image import job the field entities.image_id is already filled while the job status is still RUNNING, about 15 seconds into the job, and the status turned to SUCCESS about 260 seconds in on the recorded run. (KB-NDOR, live, checked 2026-07-30)
- Creating a whole-ECS image from a CBR backup through the IMS wholeimages API requires the field whole_image_type set to CBR (API reference). On 2026-09-25 a request with a server backup id and no whole_image_type was refused with HTTP 400 IMG.0198 (backup id does not match the backup type); in February the same case returned IMG.1061. (KB-NDXN, live, checked 2026-09-25)
- Other private images are exported with POST /v1/cloudimages/{image_id}/file to a Standard OBS bucket in the same region as VMDK, VHD, QCOW2, ZVHD or ZVHD2 files, for images smaller than 1 TiB. Images larger than 128 GiB go only through fast export, which writes ZVHD2 and needs an unencrypted image. (KB-NX4W, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- Image deletion uses the Glance-native path: DELETE /v2/images/{image_id} on the IMS endpoint returns 204, while DELETE /v2/cloudimages/{id} returns HTTP 404 with APIGW.0101 "method DELETE not found". (KB-ODE7, live, checked 2026-07-09)
- On a Windows system volume created from an image, background loading reached 100 percent after 438 s. The machine already answered when about 7 percent was loaded. (KB-ON4R, live, checked 2026-09-02)
- A second load of the same model inside the same instance runs from the OS page cache and took about 50 minutes instead of 2.5 hours. An instance newly created from an IMS image of that system starts with a cold page cache and loads at cold speed again. (KB-P6WA, live, checked 2026-05-07)
- An ECS created from a system disk image takes its flavor and system disk type from the create step rather than from the image, with a system disk at least as large as the min_disk of the image. When an ECS is created from a full-ECS image, it gets the system and data disks of the image by default and their sizes can grow but not shrink. (KB-PAHE, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- An appliance image in qcow2 format that brings its own kernel and drivers is imported into IMS with is_config false, the API default, so the import skips automatic configuration. On Linux that step, among other changes, removes PV drivers, rewrites grub and fstab to UUIDs, deletes VMware Tools services and copies VirtIO drivers into the initrd, which can misconfigure such a guest. (KB-PLD6, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- Image Management (IM) in the preview section of the service description offers the public preview image Standard_Ubuntu_20.04_AI_Frameworks_Preview, an Ubuntu 20.04 image with AI frameworks. Like every preview and beta version it is meant for testing, and Telekom advises against using it in live environments. (KB-QEH3, contract, checked 2026-10-11)
- The Glance-native route for the same cross-tenant image share is POST /v2/images/{id}/members by the owner, followed by PUT /v2/images/{id}/members/{member_id} with status accepted by the recipient. (KB-R46O, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- IMS requires VirtIO (KVM guest OS) drivers to be installed in a Windows image file on the source platform before export. Without them, ECSs created from the image fail to start. DHCP must also be configured in the image. (KB-RB2V, docs, checked 2026-10-09)
- The new image is followed with GET /v2/cloudimages?name={name} and moves through status queued, then saving, then active. For a system disk of about 80 GB, reaching active took minutes. (KB-RI7K, live, checked 2026-06-23)
- IMS requires DHCP in an imported Linux image file, otherwise ECS startup or networking is abnormal, and a source VM with a static IP address has to be switched to DHCP so that the new ECS takes its address from the subnet. (KB-RU6H, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- The IMS list of OSs supported for external image files names Windows Server 2022 as the newest Windows Server. Windows Server 2025 is not listed, so its import may fail. (KB-S6QF, docs, checked 2026-10-09)
- When automatic configuration is enabled for the registration of an image file, IMS records the latest automatic change it made to a Linux image in /var/log/rainbow_modification_record.log. On a Windows image it restores the IDE driver, regenerates the mouse and keyboard registry keys and restores DHCP, so the OS gets its IP address by DHCP. (KB-T7QX, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- A vendor appliance image published as a KVM or OpenStack x86-64 build imports through IMS and boots on TCP. (KB-TSYG, live, checked 2026-07-23)
- Creating a system-disk image from a Windows ECS with POST /v2/cloudimages/action took about 25 minutes for a 200 GB disk. (KB-TWQP, live, checked 2026-06-11)
- The IMS list of operating systems supported for imported private images has Windows 10 64bit as the newest client Windows, and Windows 11 is absent from it. Uploading an image whose OS is not on the list is documented as possibly failing, and the docs advise contacting customer service before the upload. (KB-ULTQ, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- Creating a whole machine image from a CBR backup is done with POST /v1/cloudimages/wholeimages/action and whole_image_type set to CBR. (KB-URBQ, docs, checked 2026-09-25)
- A single image is read with GET /v2/cloudimages?id={image_id} on ims.eu-de.otc.t-systems.com. The image sits in the images array, status is active once the image is usable, __image_size gives the file size in bytes as a string and min_disk is in GB. (KB-VAUK, live, checked 2026-09-25)
- An IMS import submitted without os_version registers the image with __os_version set to Other(64 bit) and virtual_env_type FusionCompute instead of ECS. Such an image still provisioned and booted on an s9 flavor, with degraded OS typing and driver handling. (KB-VESX, live, checked 2026-06-12)
- An image registered from an external file reports virtual_env_type FusionCompute and __platform Other even when os_version is passed explicitly, and instances created from it still provision and boot. (KB-W65G, live, checked 2026-09-02)
- Deleting an IMS image leaves ECS instances that were created from that image untouched and running. (KB-WK5F, live, checked 2026-07-09)
- On 2026-10-06 an ECS from the eu-de public image Standard_Ubuntu_24.04_amd64_bios_latest (image build OTC_Ubuntu_24 of 2026-09-14) ran apt against archive.ubuntu.com and security.ubuntu.com, with only the OTC Tools repository on debmirror.eu-de.otc-service.com, according to its boot log ('Using classic all-in-one repo config'). That build therefore needs internet access, through NAT or an EIP, for package installs. KB-IT7E saw the debmirror as the package source on 2026-10-01. (KB-WP5X, live, checked 2026-10-06)
- The public image Standard_Debian_12_amd64_bios_latest on T Cloud Public (TCP) eu-de logs in with the default SSH user linux rather than debian. (KB-WS3R, live, checked 2026-08-26)
- Registering an external image file as a private image takes an optional boot mode parameter with the values BIOS and UEFI. (KB-YK4H, docs, checked 2026-09-21)
